
local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local metatable = getmetatable(f)

		if type(metatable) == "table" and metatable.__iter then
			f, s, c = metatable.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local tablePack = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if tablePack[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = tablePack[1]

		return true, table.unpack(tablePack, 1, tablePack.n)
	end
end

end)

EquipWeapon = function(arg)
	if not arg then
		return
	end

	if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(arg) then
		plr.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(arg))
	end
end
weaponSc = function(arg)
	for _, child in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == arg then
			EquipWeapon(child.Name)
		end
	end
end
Attack = {}
Attack.__index = Attack
Attack.Alive = function(character)
	if not character then
		return false
	end

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	return humanoid ~= nil and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart ~= nil
end
Attack.Pos = function(arg, arg2)
	return (Root.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude <= arg2
end
Attack.Dist = function(character, arg)
	local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	return humanoidRootPart and (Root.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= arg or false
end
Attack.DistH = function(character, arg)
	local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	return humanoidRootPart and (Root.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > arg or false
end
Attack.Kill = function(character, arg)
	if character and arg then
		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not humanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		if not character:GetAttribute("Locked") then
			character:SetAttribute("Locked", humanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		end

		PosMon = character:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
		BringEnemy()
		EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)

		local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

		if (tool and tool:FindFirstChild("ToolTip") and tool.ToolTip or "") == "Blox Fruit" then
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
		else
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
		end

		if RandomCFrame then
			task.wait(0.5)
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
			task.wait(0.5)
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
			task.wait(0.5)
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
			task.wait(0.5)
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
			task.wait(0.5)
			_tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
		end
	end
end

			task.wait(0.1)
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
			task.wait(0.1)
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))

			task.wait(0.1)

		end
	end
end

end

			task.wait(0.1)

			task.wait(0.1)
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
			task.wait(0.1)
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
			task.wait(0.1)
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
		end
	end
end
Attack.Mas = function(inst, arg)
	if inst and arg then
		if not inst:GetAttribute("Locked") then
			inst:SetAttribute("Locked", inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		end

		PosMon = inst:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
		BringEnemy()

		if inst.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
			weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))

			local selectedMasterySkills = _G.SelectedMasterySkills

			if type(selectedMasterySkills) == "string" then
				selectedMasterySkills = { selectedMasterySkills }
			elseif type(selectedMasterySkills) ~= "table" then
				selectedMasterySkills = {
					"Z",
					"X",
					"C",
					"V",
					"F",
				}
			end

			for k, SelectedMasterySkill in pairs(selectedMasterySkills) do
				if type(k) == "number" then
					if type(SelectedMasterySkill) == "string" then
						Useskills("Blox Fruit", SelectedMasterySkill)

						continue
					end
				end

				if type(k) == "string" and SelectedMasterySkill == true then
					Useskills("Blox Fruit", k)
				end
			end
		else
			weaponSc("Melee")
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
		end
	end
end
Attack.Masgun = function(inst, arg)
	if inst and arg then
		if not inst:GetAttribute("Locked") then
			inst:SetAttribute("Locked", inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		end

		PosMon = inst:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
		BringEnemy()

		if inst.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
			weaponSc("Gun")
			_tp(inst.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 35, 8))

			local selectedMasterySkills = _G.SelectedMasterySkills

			if type(selectedMasterySkills) == "string" then
				selectedMasterySkills = { selectedMasterySkills }

				selectedMasterySkills = {
					"Z",

			for k, SelectedMasterySkill in pairs(selectedMasterySkills) do

						Useskills("Gun", SelectedMasterySkill)

		else

end
statsSetings = function(arg, arg2)
	if arg ~= "Melee" then
		if arg ~= "Defense" then
			if arg ~= "Sword" then
				if arg ~= "Gun" then
					if arg == "Devil" and plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Demon Fruit", arg2)
					end
				elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Gun", arg2)
				end
			elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
				replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Sword", p23)
			end
		elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
			replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Defense", p23)
		end
	elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
		replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Melee", p23)
	end
end

do
	BringEnemy = function(character)
		if not _B then
			return
		end

		if not character then
			local humanoidRootPart = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not humanoidRootPart then
				return
			end

			local mathHuge = math.huge

			for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				local humanoid = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoid and humanoidRootPart2 and humanoid.Health > 0 then
					local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

					if magnitude < mathHuge then
						mathHuge = magnitude
						character = child
					end
				end
			end

			if not character then
				return
			end
		end

		local u34 = false

		Mobs = function(character2)
			local humanoid = character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local humanoidRootPart = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			return humanoid and humanoidRootPart and humanoid.Health > 0, humanoidRootPart, humanoid
		end
		Network = function(part)
			if isnetworkowner then
				return isnetworkowner(part)
			end

			return part.ReceiveAge == 0 and not part.Anchored and part.Velocity.Magnitude > 0
		end
		pcall(function()
			if sethiddenproperty then
				sethiddenproperty(plr, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
			end

			local position = character.HumanoidRootPart.Position

			for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				if child ~= character then
					local mobs, mobs2, mobs3 = Mobs(child)

					if mobs and child.Name == character.Name then
						local magnitude = (mobs2.Position - position).Magnitude

						if magnitude <= 3000 then
							if not mobs2:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity") then
								local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")

								bodyVelocity.Name = "BodyVelocity"
								bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
								bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
								bodyVelocity.Parent = mobs2
							end

							if magnitude <= 10 then
								u34 = true
							end

							if not u34 and Network(mobs2) then
								mobs2.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
							end

							mobs2.CanCollide = false
							mobs3.WalkSpeed = 0
							mobs3.JumpPower = 0
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				character.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
				character.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
				character.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
			end
		end)
	end
end

IsSkillReady = function(arg, arg2)
	local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	local name

	if localPlayer.Character then
		for _, child in pairs(localPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == arg then
				name = child.Name

				break
			end
		end
	end

	if not name and localPlayer.Backpack then
		for _, child in pairs(localPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == arg then
				name = child.Name

				break
			end
		end
	end

	if name then
		if localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main") then
			if localPlayer.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild("Skills") then
				local child = localPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Skills:FindFirstChild(name)

				if child then
					local child2 = child:FindFirstChild(arg2)

					if child2 then
						local lock = child2:FindFirstChild("Lock") or child2:FindFirstChild("Locked")

						if lock and lock.Visible then
							return false
						end

						local cooldown = child2:FindFirstChild("Cooldown")

						if
							cooldown
							and cooldown.Visible
							and (cooldown.Size.X.Scale > 0.05 or cooldown.AbsoluteSize.X > 5)
						then
							return false
						end

						return true
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return true
end
Useskills = function(arg, arg2)
	if arg == "Melee" then
		weaponSc("Melee")

		if not IsSkillReady(arg, arg2) then
			return
		end

		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
	elseif arg == "Sword" then
		weaponSc("Sword")

		if not IsSkillReady(arg, arg2) then
			return
		end

		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
	elseif arg == "Blox Fruit" then
		weaponSc("Blox Fruit")

		if not IsSkillReady(arg, arg2) then
			return
		end

		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
	elseif arg == "Gun" then
		weaponSc("Gun")

		if not IsSkillReady(arg, arg2) then
			return
		end

		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, arg2, false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
	end

	if arg == "nil" and arg2 == "Y" then
		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Y", false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
		virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Y", false, game)
		task.wait(0.04)
	end
end

do
	local getRawMetatable = getrawmetatable(game)
	local __namecall = getRawMetatable.__namecall

	setreadonly(getRawMetatable, false)
	getRawMetatable.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
		local getNamecallMethod = getnamecallmethod()
		local tablo = { ... }

		if
			tostring(getNamecallMethod) ~= "FireServer"
			or tostring(tablo[1]) ~= "RemoteEvent"
			or not (
				tostring(tablo[2]) ~= "true"
				and tostring(tablo[2]) ~= "false"
				and not (
					(not _G.FarmMastery_G or SoulGuitar)
					and not (
						_G.FarmMastery_Dev
						or _G.FarmBlazeEM
						or _G.Prehis_Skills
						or _G.SeaBeast1
						or _G.FishBoat
						or _G.PGB
						or _G.Leviathan1
						or _G.Complete_Trials
						or not (
							(not _G.AimMethod or ABmethod ~= "Aim Player")
							and (not _G.AimMethod or ABmethod ~= "Nearest Aim")
						)
					)
				)
			)
		then
			return __namecall(...)
		end

		tablo[2] = MousePos

		return __namecall(unpack(tablo))
	end)
end

GetNearestEnemy = function(arg)
	local character = plr.Character

	character = character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not character then
		return nil
	end

	local child
	local magnitude

	for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Model") then
			if typeof(arg) == "table" and table.find(arg, child.Name) or child.Name == arg then
				local humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local humanoidRootPart = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoid and humanoidRootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 then
					local magnitude2 = (humanoidRootPart.Position - character.Position).Magnitude

					if not magnitude or magnitude2 < magnitude then
						child = child
						magnitude = magnitude2
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return v60, v61
end

do
	GetConnectionEnemies = function(arg)
		local humanoidRootPart = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		search = function(inst)
			local child
			local magnitude

			for _, child in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Model") then
					local humanoid, humanoidRootPart2, humanoidRootPart3

					if typeof(arg) == "table" then
						if table.find(arg, child.Name) then
							humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

							if not humanoid or not (humanoid.Health > 0) then
								continue
							end

							humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							humanoidRootPart3 = humanoidRootPart
									and humanoidRootPart2
									and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
								or math.huge

							if child and not (humanoidRootPart3 < magnitude) then
								continue
							end

							child = child
							magnitude = humanoidRootPart3

							continue
						end
					end

					if child.Name == arg then
						Humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

						if Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0 then
							HumanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							Magnitude = humanoidRootPart
									and HumanoidRootPart2
									and (HumanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
								or math.huge

							if not child or Magnitude < magnitude then
								child = child
								magnitude = Magnitude
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return v68
		end

		local search2 = search(workspace.Enemies) or search(replicated)

		return search2
	end
end

do
	LowCpu = function()
		local terrain = game.Workspace.Terrain

		terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
		terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
		terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
		terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
		game.Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
		game.Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
		game.Lighting.Brightness = 0
		settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"

		for _, descendant in pairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
			if
				descendant:IsA("Part")
				or descendant:IsA("Union")
				or descendant:IsA("CornerWedgePart")
				or descendant:IsA("TrussPart")
			then
				descendant.Material = "Plastic"
				descendant.Reflectance = 0
			else
				if not descendant:IsA("Decal") then
					if not descendant:IsA("Texture") then
						if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") then
							descendant.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
						elseif descendant:IsA("Explosion") then
							descendant.BlastPressure = 1
							descendant.BlastRadius = 1
						elseif
							descendant:IsA("Fire")
							or descendant:IsA("SpotLight")
							or descendant:IsA("Smoke")
							or descendant:IsA("Sparkles")
						then
							descendant.Enabled = false
						elseif descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
							descendant.Material = "Plastic"
							descendant.Reflectance = 0
							descendant.TextureID = 10385902758728956
						end

						continue
					end
				end

				descendant.Transparency = 1
			end
		end

		for _, child in pairs(game.Lighting:GetChildren()) do
			if
				child:IsA("BlurEffect")
				or child:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
				or child:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
				or child:IsA("BloomEffect")
				or child:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect")
			then
				child.Enabled = false
			end
		end
	end
end

	return true

CheckPirateGrandBrigade = function()
	local pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())
	local geniter2 = geniter(pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3)
	local v90_2

	repeat
		local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

		if not tablePack[1] then
			return false
		end

		v90_2 = tablePack[3]
	until v90_2.Name == "PirateGrandBrigade" or v90_2.Name == "PirateBrigade"

	while true do
		local exitTo

		repeat
			if v90_2:FindFirstChild("Health").Value > 0 then
				return true
			end

			exitTo = nil

			while true do
				local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

				if not tablePack[1] then
					exitTo = 1

					break
				end

				v90_2 = tablePack[3]

				if v90_2.Name == "PirateGrandBrigade" then
					exitTo = 2

					break
				elseif v90_2.Name ~= "PirateBrigade" then
					continue
				end

				break
			end

			if exitTo == 1 then
				return false
			end
		until exitTo ~= 2
	end
end

	local children, children2 = plr.Backpack:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, children2 do
		if string.find(v.Name, "Fruit") then
			EquipWeapon(v.Name)
			task.wait(0.1)

			if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
				plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
			end

			EquipWeapon(v.Name)
			plr.Character:FindFirstChild(v.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer("Drop")
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if string.find(child.Name, "Fruit") then
			EquipWeapon(child.Name)
			task.wait(0.1)

			if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
				plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
			end

			EquipWeapon(child.Name)
			plr.Character:FindFirstChild(child.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer("Drop")
		end
	end

GetInventoryList = function()
	if tablo.data and tick() - tablo.t < 2 then
		return tablo.data
	end

	local result3
	local pcallOk, invokeServer = pcall(function()
		return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
	end)

	if pcallOk and type(invokeServer) == "table" then
		result3 = invokeServer
	end

	if not result3 then
		local pcallOk2, invokeServer2 = pcall(function()
			return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventoryWeapons")
		end)

		if pcallOk2 and type(invokeServer2) == "table" then
			result3 = invokeServer2
		end
	end

	local result3_2 = result3 or {}

	tablo.t = tick()
	tablo.data = result3_2

	return result3_2
end
GetIn = function(arg)
	if not plr.Character:FindFirstChild(arg) then
		if not plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(arg) then
			for _, v in pairs(GetInventoryList()) do
				if type(v) == "table" and v.Name == arg then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end
	end

	return true
end
GetM = function(arg)
	if arg == "Bones" then
		local pcallOk, invokeServer = pcall(function()
			return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Check")
		end)
		local toNumber = pcallOk and tonumber(invokeServer) or 0

		return toNumber
	end

	local pcallOk, result = pcall(function()
		local material = require(replicated.IdMap).Material[arg]

		if material then
			return require(replicated.Util.ItemReplication).Quantity.readClient(material)
		end
	end)

	if ok and tonumber(result) then
		return tonumber(result)
	end

	for _, v in pairs(GetInventoryList()) do
		if type(v) ~= "table" or v.Type ~= "Material" or v.Name ~= p37 then
			continue
		end

		return v.Count
	end

	return 0
end
GetWP = function(arg)
	local character = not plr.Character

	if not character then
		character = not plr.Character:FindFirstChild(arg)
	end

	if not character then
		return true
	end

	if plr.Backpack then
		if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(arg) then
			return true
		end
	end

	for _, v in pairs(GetInventoryList()) do
		if type(v) == "table" and v.Type == "Sword" and v.Name == p38 then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

	t = 0,
	v = nil,

CDKProgress = function()
	if tablo2.v and tick() - tablo2.t < 0.4 then
		return tablo2.v
	end

	local function func()
		return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CDKQuest", "Progress")
	end

	local pcallOk, result = pcall(func)

	if pcallOk and type(result) == "table" then
		tablo2.v = result
		tablo2.t = tick()
	end

	return tablo2.v
end
getgenv().CDKDoorOpenedAt = nil
CDKEnsureDoor = function()
	if getgenv().CDKDoorOpenedAt then
		return true
	end

	if tick() - (getgenv().CDKDoorTry or 0) < 1 then
		return false
	end

	getgenv().CDKDoorTry = tick()

	local pcallOk, invokeServer = pcall(function()
		return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CDKQuest", "OpenDoor")
	end)

	if not pcallOk or tostring(invokeServer) ~= "opened" then
		pcall(function()
			replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CDKQuest", "OpenDoor", true)
		end)

		return false
	end

	getgenv().CDKDoorOpenedAt = tick()

	return true
end
getInfinity_Ability = function(arg, arg2)
	if not Root then
		return
	end

	if arg == "Soru" and arg2 then
		local next = next
		local getGC, getGC2 = getgc()

		for _, v in next, getGC, getGC2 do
			if plr.Character.Soru and typeof(v) == "function" then
				if getfenv(v).script == plr.Character.Soru then
					local next2 = next
					local getUpvalues, getUpvalues2 = getupvalues(v)

					for _, value in next2, getUpvalues, getUpvalues2 do
						if typeof(value) == "table" then
							repeat
								task.wait(Sec)
								value.LastUse = 0
							until plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	elseif arg == "Energy" and arg2 then
		plr.Character.Energy.Changed:connect(function()
			if arg2 then
				plr.Character.Energy.Value = Energy
			end
		end)
	elseif arg == "Observation" and arg2 then
		plr.VisionRadius.Value = math.huge
	end
end

do
	Hop = function()
		pcall(function()
			local _
			local state = 1
			local mathRandom, n1, n2, mathRandom2, forIndex, forLimit, forStep, forIndex2, t80_120_1_2, t80_120_1_3, next_, response, forIter, forState, t80_120_1_2_2, mathRandom2_2

			while true do
				if state == 1 then
					mathRandom = math.random(1, math.random(40, 75))
					n1 = 1
					n2 = 100
					mathRandom2 = mathRandom - n1
					state = 2
				elseif state == 2 then
					forIndex, forLimit, forStep = mathRandom, 100, 1

					if forStep > 0 and forIndex <= forLimit or forStep <= 0 and forIndex >= forLimit then
						forIndex2 = forIndex
						state = 5
					else
						state = 4
					end
				elseif state == 3 then
					forIndex += forStep

					if forStep > 0 and forIndex <= forLimit or forStep <= 0 and forIndex >= forLimit then
						forIndex2 = forIndex
						state = 5
					else
						state = 4
					end
				else
					if state == 4 then
						return
					end

					if state == 5 then
						local geniter2 = geniter(next, replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(forIndex2), nil)
						local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

						if tablePack[1] then
							state = 6
						else
							state = 3
						end
					elseif state == 6 then
						t80_120_1_2 = t80_120_1[2]
						t80_120_1_3 = t80_120_1[3]
						state = 7
					elseif state == 7 then
						if tonumber(t80_120_1_3.Count) < 12 then
							state = 8
						else
							state = 9
						end
					elseif state == 8 then
						TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, t80_120_1_2)
						state = 9
					elseif state == 9 then
						forIter, forState, t80_120_1_2_2 = next_, response, nil

						if type(forIter) == "table" then
							local metatable = getmetatable(forIter)

							if type(metatable) == "table" and metatable["__iter"] then
								forIter, forState, t80_120_1_2_2 = (metatable["__iter"](forIter))
							else
								forIter, forState, t80_120_1_2_2 = next, forIter, nil
							end
						end

						state = 10
					elseif state == 10 then
						t80_120_1_2, t80_120_1_3 = (forIter(forState, t80_120_1_2_2))

						if t80_120_1_2 == nil then
							state = 11
						else
							t80_120_1_2_2 = t80_120_1_2
							state = 7
						end
					elseif state == 11 then
						mathRandom2 += n1

						if n1 <= 0 then
							state = 15
						else
							state = 12
						end
					elseif state == 12 then
						if mathRandom2 <= n2 then
							state = 13
						else
							state = 14
						end
					elseif state == 13 then
						mathRandom2_2 = mathRandom2
						state = 17
					else
						if state == 14 then
							return
						end

						if state == 15 then
							if v172 >= n2 then
								state = 16
							else
								state = 18
							end
						elseif state == 16 then
							v172 = v172
							state = 17
						elseif state == 17 then
							response = replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(v172)
							next_ = next
							state = 10
						elseif state == 18 then
							return
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

		child:Destroy()
	end

local tablo3 = {
	Tween = nil,
	Target = nil,
}

getgenv().LastTPCall = 0
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if part then
			if part.Parent == workspace then
				if shouldTween then
					getgenv().OnFarm = true
				else
					getgenv().OnFarm = false
				end

				continue
			end
		end

		getgenv().OnFarm = false
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer

	while true do
		task.wait()

		if not localPlayer.Character then
			continue
		end

		if localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart then
			break
		end
	end

	part.CFrame = localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame

	while task.wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if part then
				if part.Parent == workspace then
					local primaryPart = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart

					if getgenv().OnFarm then
						if not _G.WasOnFarm then
							_G.WasOnFarm = true
						end

						local character

						if not primaryPart then
							character = localPlayer.Character

							if not character then
								return
							end

							for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("BasePart") then
									child.CanCollide = false
								end
							end
						elseif (primaryPart.Position - part.Position).Magnitude <= 200 then
							primaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position)
							character = localPlayer.Character

							if not character then
								return
							end

							for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("BasePart") then
									child.CanCollide = false
								end
							end
						elseif tablo3.Tween then
							tablo3.Tween:Cancel()
							tablo3.Target = nil
							part.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame
							character = localPlayer.Character

							if character then
								for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
									if child:IsA("BasePart") then
										child.CanCollide = false
									end
								end
							end
						else
							tablo3.Target = nil
							part.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame
							character = localPlayer.Character

							if not character then
								return
							end

							for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("BasePart") then
									child.CanCollide = false
								end
							end
						end
					else
						local character2

						if tablo3.Tween and tablo3.Tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing then
							tablo3.Tween:Cancel()
							tablo3.Target = nil

							if primaryPart then
								part.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame
							end

							if _G.WasOnFarm then
								_G.WasOnFarm = false
								character2 = localPlayer.Character

								if character2 then
									for _, child in pairs(character2:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("BasePart") then
											child.CanCollide = true
										end
									end
								end
							end
						else
							tablo3.Target = nil

							if primaryPart then
								part.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame
							end

							if _G.WasOnFarm then
								_G.WasOnFarm = false
								character2 = localPlayer.Character

								if character2 then
									for _, child in pairs(character2:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("BasePart") then
											child.CanCollide = true
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

game:GetService("TweenService")
game:GetService("RunService")

sea2 = game.PlaceId == 4442272183 or game.PlaceId == 79091703265657

	["Tween Speed"] = 200,
	["Bypass Teleport"] = false,
	["Up Y"] = false,
	["Up Y When Low Health"] = false,
	["Same Y"] = false,
}

Convert_CFrame = function(part2)
	if not part2 then
		return
	end

	if typeof(part2) == "Vector3" then
		return CFrame.new(part2)
	end

	if typeof(part2) == "CFrame" then
		return part2
	end

	if typeof(part2) == "Model" then
		return part2:GetPivot()
	end

	if part2.CFrame then
		return part2.CFrame
	end

	return nil
end
GetDistance = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	if arg == nil then
		return 9e9
	end

	local character = localPlayer.Character

	if not character then
		return 9e9
	end

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return 9e9
	end

	if arg2 == nil then
		arg2 = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not arg2 then
			return 9e9
		end
	end

	local convert_CFrame = Convert_CFrame(arg)
	local convert_CFrame2 = Convert_CFrame(arg2)

	if arg3 then
		return (Vector3.new(convert_CFrame.X, 0, convert_CFrame.Z) - Vector3.new(convert_CFrame2.X, 0, convert_CFrame2.Z)).Magnitude
	end

	return (convert_CFrame.Position - convert_CFrame2.Position).Magnitude
end
InArea = function(arg)
	local _WorldOrigin = workspace:FindFirstChild("_WorldOrigin")

	if not _WorldOrigin then
		return {
			Name = "",
		}
	end

	local convert_CFrame = Convert_CFrame(arg)
	local children, children2 = _WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, children2 do
		if v:FindFirstChild("Mesh") and (convert_CFrame.Position - v.Position).Magnitude <= v.Mesh.Scale.X then
			return v
		end
	end

	return {
		Name = "",
	}
end
GetSpawnPoint = function(arg)
	local worldOrigin = workspace:FindFirstChild("_WorldOrigin")
		and workspace._WorldOrigin:FindFirstChild("PlayerSpawns")
		and workspace._WorldOrigin.PlayerSpawns:FindFirstChild("Pirates")

	if not worldOrigin then
		return
	end

	local children, children2 = worldOrigin:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, children2 do
		if not v:FindFirstChild("Part") then
			continue
		end

		if (v.Part.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude <= 2500 then
			return v
		end
	end
end
CheckLegendaryItems = function()
	CheckItem = function(arg)
		local children, children3 = localPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()

		for _, v in next, children, children3 do
			if v:IsA("Tool") and (v.Name == arg or string.find(v.Name, arg)) then
				return v
			end
		end

		local children2, children4 = localPlayer.Character:GetChildren()

		for _, v in next, children2, children4 do
			if v:IsA("Tool") and (v.Name == arg or string.find(v.Name, arg)) then
				return v
			end
		end
	end

	if
		CheckItem("God's Chalice")
		or CheckItem("Fist of Darkness")
		or CheckItem("Sweet Chalice")
		or CheckItem("Hallow Essence")
		or CheckItem("Flower1")
	then
		return true
	end

	return false
end
WaitForHumanoid = function()
	local character = localPlayer.Character

	if not character then
		return nil
	end

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if humanoid then
		return humanoid
	end

	local tick2 = tick() + 5

	while tick() < tick2 do
		local humanoid2 = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

		if humanoid2 then
			return humanoid2
		end

		task.wait(0.1)
	end

	return nil
end
checkinventory = function(arg)
	if arg then
		for _, v in pairs(GetInventoryList()) do
			if type(v) ~= "table" then
				continue
			end

			if v.Name == arg then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end
getdis = function(arg, arg2)
	local arg2_2 = arg2 or localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame

	return (CFrame.new(arg.X, arg2_2.Y, arg.Z).Position - CFrame.new(arg2_2.X, arg2_2.Y, arg2_2.Z).Position).Magnitude
end
CanBypassTeleport = function(arg)
	local name = InArea(arg).Name

	if name == "" then
		return false
	end

	if
		not (not tablo4["Bypass Teleport"] or name:find("Dimension") or name:find("Submerged") or name == "Sealed Cavern")
	then
		if not (name:lower():find("under") or CheckLegendaryItems()) then
			if
				localPlayer.Data
				and localPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint
				and localPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint.Value == "SubmergedIsland"
			then
				return false
			end

			if GetDistance(arg.Position) <= 3500 then
				return false
			end

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end
GetBypassCFrame = function(arg)
	local mathHuge = math.huge
	local v
	local children = workspace._WorldOrigin.PlayerSpawns.Pirates:GetChildren()
	local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not humanoidRootPart then
		return nil
	end

	for _, v in next, children, nil do
		if
			v:FindFirstChild("Part")
			and (arg.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude >= 3000
			and GetSpawnPoint(v.Part) ~= GetSpawnPoint(humanoidRootPart)
			and (v.Part.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10000
			and (v.Part.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude <= mathHuge
		then
			mathHuge = (v.Part.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude
			v = v
		end
	end

	return v
end
BypassTP = function(arg)
	local character = localPlayer.Character

	if not character then
		return
	end

	local waitForHumanoid = WaitForHumanoid()

	if not waitForHumanoid or waitForHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end

	if CanBypassTeleport(arg) and GetBypassCFrame(arg) then
		local getBypassCFrame = GetBypassCFrame(arg)

		if getBypassCFrame and getBypassCFrame:FindFirstChild("Part") then
			character.LastSpawnPoint.Disabled = true
			replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("SetLastSpawnPoint", getBypassCFrame.Name)
			replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("SetSpawnPoint")
			character:PivotTo(getBypassCFrame.Part.CFrame)
			waitForHumanoid:ChangeState(15)

			while true do
				task.wait()

				if not localPlayer.Character then
					continue
				end

				if WaitForHumanoid() and WaitForHumanoid().Health > 0 then
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
totopofgreattree = function()
	if getdis(CFrame.new(28310.0234, 14895.1123, 109.456741)) > 1500 then
		replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
			"requestEntrance",
			Vector3.new(28310.0234, 14895.1123, 109.456741)
		)
		task.wait(0.3)
	end

	local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(28607.5352, 14896.5449, 106.011726)

	_tp(cFrame3)

	repeat
		task.wait()
	until getdis(cFrame3) <= 5

	task.wait(0.5)

	for i = 1, 4 do
		replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "TeleportBack")
	end
end
requestentrance = function(arg)
	local arg2 = arg

	if typeof(arg) == "CFrame" then
		arg2 = arg.Position
	end

	local t6

	if sea1 then
		t6 = {
			Sky3 = Vector3.new(-7894, 5547, -380),
			Sky3Exit = Vector3.new(-4607, 874, -1667),
			UnderWater = Vector3.new(61163, 11, 1819),
			["Underwater City"] = Vector3.new(61165.19140625, 0.18704631924629211, 1897.379150390625),
			["Pirate Village"] = Vector3.new(-1242.4625244140625, 4.7870597839355469, 3901.282958984375),
			UnderwaterExit = Vector3.new(4050, -1, -1814),
		}
	elseif sea2 then
		t6 = {
			["Swan Mansion"] = Vector3.new(-390, 332, 673),
			["Swan Room"] = Vector3.new(2285, 15, 905),
			["Cursed Ship"] = Vector3.new(923, 126, 32852),
			["Zombie Island"] = Vector3.new(-6509, 83, -133),
		}
	else
		t6 = {
			["Hydra Island"] = Vector3.new(5657.88623046875, 1013.0790405273438, -335.4996337890625),
			Mansion = Vector3.new(-12462, 375, -7552),
			Castle = Vector3.new(-5036, 315, -3179),
			["Temple of Time"] = Vector3.new(28286, 14897, 103),
			["Greate Tree"] = Vector3.new(3024.1709, 2280.69434, -7325.12793),
		}

		if not checkinventory("Valkyrie Helm") then
			return
		end
	end

	local v
	local mathHuge = math.huge

	for _, v in pairs(t6) do
		local magnitude = typeof(v) == "Vector3" and (v - arg2).Magnitude or (v.Position - arg2).Magnitude

		if magnitude < mathHuge then
			mathHuge = magnitude
			v = v
		end
	end

	if v then
		if mathHuge and mathHuge < getdis(arg) then
			pcall(function()
				if _G.TweenCache then
					_G.TweenCache:Cancel()
				end
			end)

			local ok = typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" or v.X ~= 3024.1709 or v.Y ~= 2280.69434 or v.Z ~= -7325.12793

			if not ok then
				ok = not (replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Check") >= 2)
			end

			if ok then
				if mathHuge < getdis(arg) then
					replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
						"requestEntrance",
						typeof(v) == "Vector3" and v or v.Position
					)
					task.wait(1)
				end
			else
				totopofgreattree()
				task.wait(1)
			end
		end
	end
end
_tp = function(model)
	local cFrame3

	if typeof(model) == "Vector3" then
		cFrame3 = CFrame.new(model)
	elseif typeof(model) == "CFrame" then
		cFrame3 = model
	elseif typeof(model) == "Instance" then
		local pcallOk, cFrame4 = pcall(function()
			if model:IsA("Model") then
				return model:GetPivot()
			end

			return model.CFrame
		end)
		local cFrame4_2 = pcallOk and cFrame4

		cFrame3 = cFrame4_2 or nil
	end

	if not cFrame3 then
		return
	end

	if plr.Character then
		if plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			if not part or part.Parent ~= workspace then
				return
			end

			getgenv().LastTPCall = tick()
			shouldTween = true
			getgenv().OnFarm = true

			if
				tablo3.Tween
				and tablo3.Tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing
				and tablo3.Target
				and (tablo3.Target - cFrame3.Position).Magnitude < 1
			then
				return tablo3.Tween
			end

			if sea3 then
				if getdis(cFrame3.Position, cFrame.Position) < 4000 or cFrame3.Position.Y < -1000 then
					local humanoidRootPart = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart

					if
						not (
							typeof(IsInSubmergedIsland) == "function" and IsInSubmergedIsland()
							or humanoidRootPart.Position.Y < -1000
						)
					then
						if not (getdis(cFrame2) > 15) then
							local net = replicatedStorage.Modules.Net

							pcall(function()
								net["RF/SubmarineWorkerSpeak"]:InvokeServer("AskKilledTikiBoss")
								task.wait(0.5)
								net["RF/SubmarineWorkerSpeak"]:InvokeServer("TravelToSubmergedIsland")
							end)
							task.wait(1)

							return
						end

						cFrame3 = cFrame2
					end
				end
			end

			local character = plr.Character
			local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not humanoidRootPart or not part or part.Parent ~= workspace then
				return
			end

			if tablo3.Tween then
				tablo3.Tween:Cancel()
			end

			part.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame

			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid and humanoid.Sit == true then
				part.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position.X, cFrame3.Y, part.Position.Z)
			end

			local magnitude = (cFrame3.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
			local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(
				magnitude / (magnitude <= 15 and (getgenv().TweenSpeedNear or 180) or getgenv().TweenSpeedFar or 150),
				Enum.EasingStyle.Linear
			)
			local created = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(part, tweenInfo, {
				CFrame = CFrame.new(cFrame3.Position),
			})

			tablo3.Tween = created
			tablo3.Target = cFrame3.Position
			created:Play()

			return created
		end
	end
end
old_tp = function(arg)
	if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = arg
	end
end
TeleportToTarget = function(arg)
	local _tp2 = _tp(arg)

	if _tp2 then
		_tp2.Completed:Wait()
	end

	task.wait(0.1)
end

end

	BTP = function(arg)
		local localPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

		local humanoid2 = localPlayer2.Character.Humanoid
		local main = localPlayer2.PlayerGui.Main
		local position = humanoidRootPart2.Position

		repeat
			humanoid2.Health = 0
			humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = arg
			main.Quest.Visible = false

			if (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude > 1 then
				position = humanoidRootPart2.Position
				humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = arg
			end

			task.wait(0.5)
		until (arg.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude <= 2000
	end
end

					end
				end
			elseif tick() - (getgenv().LastTPCall or 0) < 0.35 then
				shouldTween = true

				if not plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
					bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
					bodyVelocity.Name = "BodyClip"
					bodyVelocity.Parent = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
					bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
					bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end

				if not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("highlight") then
					highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
					highlight.Name = "highlight"
					highlight.Enabled = true
					highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 254)
					highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 254)
					highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
					highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.2
					highlight.Parent = plr.Character
				end

				for _, descendant in pairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.CanCollide = false
					end
				end
			else
				shouldTween = false

				if plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BodyClip"):Destroy()
				end

				if plr.Character:FindFirstChild("highlight") then
					plr.Character:FindFirstChild("highlight"):Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

do
	local module = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Quests"))
	local module2 = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("GuideModule"))
	local tablo5 = {
		"MarineQuest",
		"BartiloQuest",
		"CitizenQuest",
		"Trainees",
	}

	CheckSea = function(arg)
		if (game.PlaceId == 2753915549 or game.PlaceId == 85211729168715) and arg == 1 then
			return true
		end

		if (game.PlaceId == 4442272183 or game.PlaceId == 79091703265657) and arg == 2 then
			return true
		end

		if (game.PlaceId == 7449423635 or game.PlaceId == 100117331123089) and arg == 3 then
			return true
		end

		return false
	end
	GetQuestPointFromNPC = function(arg)
		for _, child in pairs(workspace.NPCs:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name ~= arg then
				continue
			end

			if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				return child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			end
		end

		for _, child in pairs(replicated.NPCs:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == arg and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				return child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			end
		end

		return nil
	end
	GetQuests = function()
		local value = plr.Data.Level.Value
		local levelReq = 0
		local tablo6 = {}

		if value >= 700 and CheckSea(1) then
			tablo6.Mob = "Galley Captain"
			tablo6.NameQuest = "FountainQuest"
			tablo6.ID = 2
			tablo6.LevelReq = 700
		elseif value >= 1500 and CheckSea(2) then
			tablo6.Mob = "Water Fighter"
			tablo6.NameQuest = "ForgottenQuest"
			tablo6.ID = 2
			tablo6.LevelReq = 1450
		else
			for k, Quest in pairs(module) do
				for key, v in pairs(Quest) do
					local levelReq2 = v.LevelReq

					for key2 in pairs(v.Task) do
						if levelReq2 <= value and levelReq <= levelReq2 and v.Task[key2] > 1 and not table.find(tablo5, k) then
							levelReq = levelReq2
							tablo6.Mob = key2
							tablo6.NameQuest = k
							tablo6.ID = key
							tablo6.LevelReq = levelReq2
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return tablo6
	end
	GetQuestPoint = function()
		if module2 and module2.Data then
			if module2.Data.LastClosestNPC then
				return GetQuestPointFromNPC(module2.Data.LastClosestNPC)
			end
		end

		return nil
	end
end

			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(-5815, 84, 8820)
			SP = "Default"
		elseif SelectMaterial == "Fish Tail" then
			MMon = {
				"Fishman Warrior",
				"Fishman Commando",
				"Fishman Lord",
			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(61123, 19, 1569)
			SP = "Default"
			shouldRequestEntrance(Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.3423423767089844, 1819.7841796875), 17000)
		end
	end
end

	return {

		getQuests.ID,
		getQuests.NameQuest,
		getQuests.LevelReq,

	}
end
alreadyTeleported = false
teleporting = false
_G.CheckQuestVisible = function(arg)
	local arg2 = arg or game.Players.LocalPlayer
	local quest = arg2.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main") and arg2.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild("Quest")
	local trackedQuestFrame = arg2.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TrackedQuestFrame")

	if quest and quest.Visible then
		local text = ""

		pcall(function()
			text = quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text
		end)

		return true, text
	end

	if not trackedQuestFrame or not trackedQuestFrame.Enabled then
		return false, ""
	end

	local text = ""

	pcall(function()
		text = trackedQuestFrame.Frame.header.textLabel.Text
	end)

	if text == "" then
		pcall(function()
			text = trackedQuestFrame.Frame.header.textLabel.textLabel.Text
		end)
	end

	return true, text
end
IsInSubmergedIsland = function()
	if not plr.Character then
		return false
	end

	local humanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if humanoidRootPart2 then
		return humanoidRootPart2.Position.Y < -1000
	end

	return false
end

do
	GuiNode = function(...)
		local main = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main")

		if not main then
			return nil
		end

		local main2 = main

		for _, v in ipairs({ ... }) do
			if main2 then
				main2 = main2:FindFirstChild(v)

				continue
			end

			return nil
		end

		return main2
	end
end

do
	GuiShown = function(...)
		if select("#", ...) == 1 and select(1, ...) == "Quest" then
			local trackedQuestFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TrackedQuestFrame")

			if trackedQuestFrame then
				local frame = trackedQuestFrame:FindFirstChild("Frame")

				if frame then
					return trackedQuestFrame.Enabled and frame.Visible
				end
			end
		end

		local guiNode = GuiNode(...)

		return guiNode ~= nil and guiNode.Visible == true
	end
end

QuestText = function()
	local trackedQuestFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TrackedQuestFrame")

	if trackedQuestFrame then
		local frame = trackedQuestFrame:FindFirstChild("Frame")

		if frame and frame.Visible then
			local header = frame:FindFirstChild("header")
			local textLabel = header and header:FindFirstChild("textLabel")

			if textLabel and textLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then
				return tostring(textLabel.Text)
			end
		end
	end

	local guiNode = GuiNode("Quest", "Container", "QuestTitle", "Title")

	if guiNode then
		return tostring(guiNode.Text)
	end

	return ""
end
CheckHasQuest = function(arg)
	if not arg then
		return false
	end

	if not GuiShown("Quest") then
		return false
	end

	local questText = QuestText()

	if questText == "" then
		return false
	end

	return string.find(string.lower(questText), string.lower(arg), 1, true) ~= nil
end
BFAcceptQuest = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	if CheckHasQuest(arg3) then
		return true
	end

	local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
		and game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes:FindFirstChild("CommF_")

	if not remotes then
		return false
	end

	local pcallOk = pcall(function()
		return remotes:InvokeServer("StartQuest", arg, arg2)
	end)

	if not pcallOk then
		return false
	end

	local osClock = os.clock() + 3

	while true do
		if not CheckHasQuest(arg3) then
			task.wait(0.25)

			if not (osClock <= os.clock()) then
				continue
			end

			return CheckHasQuest(arg3)
		end

		break
	end

	return true
end
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.5) do
		pcall(function()
			local chooseWP = _G.ChooseWP or "Melee"
			local name

			if plr.Character then
				for _, child in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == chooseWP then
						name = child.Name

						break
					end
				end
			end

			if not name and plr.Backpack then
				for _, child in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") then
						if child.ToolTip == chooseWP then
							name = child.Name

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if Name then
				_G.SelectWeapon = Name
			end
		end)
	end
end)

					if not character2 then

						local child

							local humanoid2 = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - vector3).Magnitude <= 700
							then
								local magnitude3 = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - character2.Position).Magnitude

								if not magnitude2 or magnitude3 < magnitude2 then
									child = child
									magnitude2 = magnitude3
								end
							end
						end

						if child then
							local tick2 = tick() + 30

							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.Kill(child, _G.AutoRaidCastle)

								if not _G.AutoRaidCastle then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or tick2 < tick() then
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(cFrame3)
						end
					else
						_tp(cFrame3)
					end
				end)
				exitTo = nil

				while task.wait(Sec) do
					if _G.AutoRaidCastle then
						exitTo = 1

						break
					end
				end
			until exitTo ~= 1

			return
		end
	end

				if not character2 then
					return
				end

				local position = character2:GetPivot().Position
				local tagged = collectionService:GetTagged("_ChestTagged")
				local mathHuge = math.huge
				local v361

				for i = 1, #tagged do
					local taggedInstance = tagged[i]
					local magnitude2 = (taggedInstance:GetPivot().Position - position).Magnitude

					if
						(not SelectedIsland or taggedInstance:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland))
						and not taggedInstance:GetAttribute("IsDisabled")
						and magnitude2 < mathHuge
					then
						mathHuge = magnitude2
						v361 = taggedInstance
					end
				end

				if v361 then
					_tp(v361:GetPivot())
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(Sec) do
		if _G.AutoBerryH then
			local collectionService2 = game:GetService("CollectionService")
			local localPlayer3 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			local tagged = collectionService2:GetTagged("BerryBush")

			if #tagged == 0 then
				local teleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
				local games = {}
				local games2 = pcall(function()
					games = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(
						game:HttpGet(
							"https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"
								.. game.PlaceId
								.. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
						)
					)
				end)

				if games2 then
					if games.data then
						for _, v in pairs(games.data) do
							if v.playing < v.maxPlayers and v.id ~= game.JobId then
								teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v.id, localPlayer3)

								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			else
				for i = 1, #tagged do
					local taggedInstance = tagged[i]

					for _, v in pairs(taggedInstance:GetAttributes()) do
						if BerryArray then
							if not table.find(BerryArray, v) then
								continue
							end
						end

						_tp(taggedInstance.Parent:GetPivot())

						for index = 1, #tagged do
							for _, child in pairs(tagged[index]:GetChildren()) do
								if BerryArray then
									if not table.find(BerryArray, child.Name) then
										continue
									end
								end

								_tp(child.WorldPivot)
								fireproximityprompt(child.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

			pcall(function()
				if game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:FindFirstChild(getgenv().SelectMob) then
					for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == getgenv().SelectMob then
							if
								child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
								and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								and child.Humanoid.Health > 0
							then
								while true do
									game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Wait()
									Attack.Kill(child, _G.AutoKillMob)

									if not _G.AutoKillMob then
										break
									elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

Core.Island = World1 and Sea1_Islands or World2 and Sea2_Islands or World3 and Sea3_Islands or {}
IslandData = Core.Island
getgenv().IslandData = IslandData
Core.GetMobs = function()
	local tablo5 = {}
	local islandData = IslandData
		or Core.Island
		or World1 and Sea1_Islands
		or World2 and Sea2_Islands
		or World3 and Sea3_Islands
		or {}

	for _, v in pairs(islandData) do
		if v.Mobs then
			for _, mob in ipairs(v.Mobs) do
				if not table.find(tablo5, mob) then
					table.insert(tablo5, mob)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(tablo5)

	return tablo5
end
getgenv().GetMobs = Core.GetMobs
task.spawn(function()
	local islandData

	while true do
		if not task.wait(0.2) then
			return
		elseif _G.AutoFarmIsland and _G.SelectIsland and IslandData then
			islandData = IslandData[_G.SelectIsland]

			if islandData then
				break
			end
		end
	end

	local exitTo

	repeat
		local cFrame3 = v430.CFrame
		local tablo5 = {}

		for _, mob in ipairs(v430.Mobs) do
			tablo5[mob] = true
		end

		local v436 = false

		for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
			if
				tablo5[child.Name]
				and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				and child.Humanoid.Health > 0
			then
				v436 = true

				while true do
					task.wait()
					_tp(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
					Attack.Kill(child, true)

					if not _G.AutoFarmIsland then
						break
					elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if not v436 then
			_tp(cFrame3)
		end

		exitTo = nil

		while task.wait(0.2) do
			if _G.AutoFarmIsland and _G.SelectIsland and IslandData then
				islandData = IslandData[_G.SelectIsland]

				if islandData then
					exitTo = 1

					break
				end
			end
		end
	until exitTo ~= 1
end)

do
	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(1) do
			pcall(function()
				if _G.FarmEliteHunt then
					local guiShown = GuiShown("Quest")
					local questText = QuestText()

					if guiShown then
						local v447
						local pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3 = pairs({
							"Diablo",
							"Urban",
							"Deandre",
						})
						local geniter2 = geniter(pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3)

						while true do
							local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

							if not tablePack[1] then
								break
							end

							local tablePack2 = tablePack[3]

							if string.find(questText, tablePack2) then
								v447 = tablePack2

								break
							end
						end

						if v441 then
							local v454
							local pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3 = pairs(replicated:GetChildren())
							local geniter2 = geniter(pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3)

							while true do
								local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

								if not tablePack[1] then
									break
								end

								local tablePack2 = tablePack[3]

								if tablePack2.Name == v441 and tablePack2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
									v454 = tablePack2

									break
								end
							end

							if not v448 then
								local pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3 = pairs(Enemies:GetChildren())

								for _, v in pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3 do
									if v.Name == v441 and Attack.Alive(v) then
										v2 = v

										break
									end
								end
							end

							if v2 and v2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								_tp(v2.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))

								while true do
									task.wait()
									Attack.Kill(v2, _G.FarmEliteHunt)

									if not _G.FarmEliteHunt then
										break
									elseif not v2.Parent or v2.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not GuiShown("Quest") then
										break
									end
								end
							else
								task.wait(5)
							end
						else
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
						end
					else
						local invokeServer = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("EliteHunter")

						if invokeServer == nil or string.find(tostring(invokeServer), "Cooldown") then
							task.wait(10)

							return
						end

						task.wait(1)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

HopServer = function()
	local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local teleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local nextPageCursor = ""
	local v464 = false

	repeat
		local games, games2 = pcall(function()
			return game:HttpGet(
				"https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"
					.. game.PlaceId
					.. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&cursor="
					.. nextPageCursor
			)
		end)

		if games and games2 then
			local decoded = httpService:JSONDecode(games2)

			if decoded.data then
				for _, v in pairs(decoded.data) do
					if v.playing < v.maxPlayers and v.id ~= game.JobId then
						v464 = true
						teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v.id)

						break
					end
				end

				nextPageCursor = decoded.nextPageCursor or ""
			end
		end
	until not nextPageCursor or v464
end

task.spawn(function()
	local vector3 = Vector3.new(-5097.93164, 316.447021, -3142.66602)
	local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-5344.822265625, 423.98541259766, -2725.0930175781)
	local tick2 = 0
	local taskWait = task.wait
	local sec = Sec

	while taskWait(sec) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.AutoRipIngay then
				local getConnectionEnemies = GetConnectionEnemies({
					"rip_indra",
					"rip_indra True Form",
				})

				if getConnectionEnemies then
					local tick3 = tick() + 120

					while true do
						task.wait()
						Attack.Kill(getConnectionEnemies, _G.AutoRipIngay)

						if not _G.AutoRipIngay then
							break
						elseif not getConnectionEnemies.Parent or getConnectionEnemies.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or tick3 < tick() then
							break
						end
					end
				else
					local humanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if
						humanoidRootPart2
						and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - cFrame3.Position).Magnitude > 400
						and tick() - tick2 > 5
					then
						tick2 = tick()
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", vector3)
						task.wait(0.1)
					end

					_tp(cFrame3)
				end
			end
		end)
		taskWait = task.wait
		sec = Sec
	end
end)
AuraSkin = function(arg)
	local tablo5 = { {
		StorageName = arg,
		Type = "AuraSkin",
		Context = "Equip",
	} }

	replicated
		:WaitForChild("Modules")
		:WaitForChild("Net")
		:WaitForChild("RF/FruitCustomizerRF")
		:InvokeServer(unpack(tablo5))
end
VaildColor = function(part2)
	if part2 then
		if part2.BrickColor then
			return tostring(part2.BrickColor) == "Lime green"
		end
	end
end
HakiCalculate = function(part2)
	local tablo5 = {
		["Really red"] = "Pure Red",
		Oyster = "Snow White",
		["Hot pink"] = "Winter Sky",
	}

	if part2 and part2.BrickColor then
		return tablo5[tostring(part2.BrickColor)]
	end
end
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(Sec) do
		if _G.AutoUnHaki then
			pcall(function()
				local summoner = workspace.Map["Boat Castle"]:FindFirstChild("Summoner")

				if summoner and summoner:FindFirstChild("Circle") then
					local pairs = pairs
					local circle = summoner:FindFirstChild("Circle")

					for _, child in pairs(circle:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "Part" then
							local part2 = child:FindFirstChild("Part")

							if VaildColor(part2) == false then
								AuraSkin(HakiCalculate(child))

								while true do
									task.wait()
									_tp(child.CFrame)

									if VaildColor(part2) == true then
										break
									elseif not _G.AutoUnHaki then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

									and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									end
								end

							end

							if

						end

							exitTo = 1

							break

					end

Check_Eye = function()
	local islandModel = workspace.Map.TikiOutpost.IslandModel
	local n7 = 0

	for _, v in ipairs({
		islandModel.Eye1,
		islandModel.Eye2,
		islandModel.IslandChunks.E.Eye3,
		islandModel.IslandChunks.E.Eye4,
	}) do
		if v and v.Transparency ~= 1 then
			n7 += 1
		end
	end

	return n7, n7 == 4
end
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(Sec) do
		if _G.FarmTyrant then
			local exitTo

			repeat
				pcall(function()
					if not plr.Character then
						return
					end

					local humanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if not humanoidRootPart2 then
						return
					end

					local vector3 = Vector3.new(-16268.287, 152.616, 1390.773)

					if (humanoidRootPart2.Position - vector3).Magnitude > 5 then
						_tp(CFrame.new(vector3))

						while true do
							task.wait()

							if not _G.FarmTyrant then
								break
							end

							if not plr.Character then
								continue
							elseif
								not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								or not ((plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector3).Magnitude <= 5)
							then
								continue
							end

							break
						end
					end

					local tyrantOfTheSkies = workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies")

					if
						tyrantOfTheSkies
						and tyrantOfTheSkies:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
						and tyrantOfTheSkies.Humanoid.Health > 0
					then
						while _G.FarmTyrant do
							if Attack and Attack.Kill then
								Attack.Kill(tyrantOfTheSkies, _G.FarmTyrant)
							end

							task.wait()

							if
								not _G.FarmTyrant
								or not tyrantOfTheSkies.Parent
								or tyrantOfTheSkies.Humanoid.Health <= 0
							then
								break
							end
						end

						return
					end

					for _, v in ipairs({
						"Serpent Hunter",
						"Skull Slayer",
						"Isle Champion",
						"Sun-kissed Warrior",
					}) do
						if not _G.FarmTyrant then
							break
						end

						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if not _G.FarmTyrant then
								break
							end

							if
								child
								and child.Name == v
								and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
								and child.Humanoid.Health > 0
							then
								if (humanoidRootPart2.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 5000 then
									_tp(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))

									local tick2 = tick()

									repeat
										task.wait()
										humanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character
											and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									until not _G.FarmTyrant
										or not humanoidRootPart2
										or (humanoidRootPart2.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 6
										or tick() - tick2 > 8
								end

								while _G.FarmTyrant do
									if Attack and Attack.Kill then
										Attack.Kill(child, _G.FarmTyrant)
									end

									task.wait()

									if not _G.FarmTyrant or not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				exitTo = nil

				while task.wait(Sec) do
					if _G.FarmTyrant then
						exitTo = 1

						break
					end
				end
			until exitTo ~= 1

			return
		end
	end
end)
sendSkillKey = function(arg)
	local virtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

	virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, arg, false, game)
	task.wait(0.05)
	virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, arg, false, game)
end
equipAndUseSkill = function(arg)
	if not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or not (plr.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
		return
	end

	for _, child in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == arg then
			child.Parent = plr.Character
			task.wait(0.12)

			for _, v in ipairs({
				"Z",
				"X",
				"C",
				"V",
				"F",
			}) do
				if _G.FarmPhaBinh then
					pcall(function()
						sendSkillKey(v)
					end)
					task.wait(0.12)
				else
					break
				end
			end

			child.Parent = plr.Backpack

			return
		end
	end
end

									end

										v577 = true

									equipAndUseSkill("Melee")

do
	task.spawn(function()
		processEnemy = function(character2, arg)
			if
				character2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				and character2.Humanoid.Health > 0
			then
				if character2.Name == arg then
					while true do
						task.wait()
						Attack.Kill(character2, getgenv().AutoMaterial)

						if not getgenv().AutoMaterial or not character2.Parent then
							break
						elseif character2.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		handleEnemySpawns = function()
			for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.EnemySpawns:GetChildren()) do
				for _, v in ipairs(MMon) do
					if string.find(child.Name, v) then
						if
							(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - child.Position).Magnitude
							>= 10
						then
							_tp(child.CFrame * Pos)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		while task.wait() do
			if getgenv().AutoMaterial then
				pcall(function()
					if getgenv().SelectMaterial then
						MaterialMon(getgenv().SelectMaterial)
						_tp(MPos)
					end

					for _, v in ipairs(MMon) do
						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							processEnemy(child, v)
						end
					end

					handleEnemySpawns()
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
								if child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and table.find(BossList, child.Name) then
									local magnitude2 = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

									if magnitude2 < mathHuge then
										child = child
										mathHuge = magnitude2
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if v597 and v597:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local humanoidRootPart2 = v597.HumanoidRootPart

									else
										_tp(cFrame3)
									end

										Attack.Kill(v597, true)
									end

									if not v597.Parent or humanoid2.Health <= 0 or not _G.AutoFarmAllBoss then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end

RunSer.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	pcall(function()
		if _G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.FarmMastery_G or _G.FarmMastery_S then
			for _, child in pairs(plr.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "NotificationTemplate" and string.find(child.Text, "Skill locked!") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end)

local function func(arg)
	if arg == "Cake" then
		return GetConnectionEnemies(mastery1), CFrame.new(-1943.676513671875, 251.50956726074219, -12337.880859375)
	end

	if arg == "Bone" then
		return GetConnectionEnemies(mastery2), CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125)
	end

	if arg == "Selected Mob" then
		if getgenv().SelectMob then
			local selectMob = getgenv().SelectMob
			local getConnectionEnemies = GetConnectionEnemies({ selectMob })
			local cFrame3

			if not getConnectionEnemies and IslandData then
				for _, v in pairs(IslandData) do
					if v.Mobs and table.find(v.Mobs, selectMob) and v.CFrame then
						cFrame3 = v.CFrame

						break
					end
				end
			end

			return getConnectionEnemies, cFrame3
		end
	end

	if World3 then
		return GetConnectionEnemies(mastery1), CFrame.new(-1943.676513671875, 251.50956726074219, -12337.880859375)
	end

	if getgenv().SelectMob then
		return GetConnectionEnemies({ getgenv().SelectMob }), nil
	end

	return nil, nil
end

						if (_G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.Auto_Fruit_Mastery) and not (v613.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
							if v613.Parent then
								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end
				elseif v614 then
					_tp(v614)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

				if func2 then

					local exitTo

						task.wait()

						Attack.Masgun(func2, _G.FarmMastery_G or _G.Auto_Gun_Mastery)

						local modules = replicated:FindFirstChild("Modules")
						local net2 = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Net")

						net = net2 and net2:FindFirstChild("RE/ShootGunEvent")

						local tool = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

						if not tool or tool.ToolTip ~= "Gun" then
							if
								(_G.FarmMastery_G or _G.Auto_Gun_Mastery)
								and not (func2.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
								and func2.Parent
							then
								continue
							end
						elseif tool.Name == "Skull Guitar" then
							SoulGuitar = true
							tool.RemoteEvent:FireServer("TAP", MousePos)
							virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
							task.wait(0.05)
							virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
							task.wait(0.05)

							if
								not (
									(_G.FarmMastery_G or _G.Auto_Gun_Mastery)
									and not (func2.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not func2.Parent)
								)
							then
								break
							end
						else
							SoulGuitar = false

							if net then
								exitTo = 1

								break
							else
								virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
								task.wait(0.05)
								virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
								task.wait(0.05)

								if
									not (
										(_G.FarmMastery_G or _G.Auto_Gun_Mastery)
										and not (func2.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not func2.Parent)
									)
								then
									break
								end
							end
						end

						break
					end

					if exitTo == 1 then
						local exitTo3

						repeat
							REShootGunEvent:FireServer(MousePos, { func2.HumanoidRootPart })
							exitTo3 = nil

							while true do
								virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
								task.wait(0.05)
								virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
								task.wait(0.05)

								local exitTo2

								while
									(_G.FarmMastery_G or _G.Auto_Gun_Mastery)
									and not (func2.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
									and func2.Parent
								do
									task.wait()
									MousePos = func2.HumanoidRootPart.Position
									Attack.Masgun(func2, _G.FarmMastery_G or _G.Auto_Gun_Mastery)

									local modules = replicated:FindFirstChild("Modules")
									local net = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Net")

									REShootGunEvent = net and net:FindFirstChild("RE/ShootGunEvent")

									local tool = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

									if tool and tool.ToolTip == "Gun" then
										if tool.Name ~= "Skull Guitar" then
											exitTo2 = 1

											break
										end

										SoulGuitar = true
										tool.RemoteEvent:FireServer("TAP", MousePos)
										virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
										task.wait(0.05)
										virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
										task.wait(0.05)
									end
								end

								if exitTo2 ~= 1 then
									break
								end

								SoulGuitar = false

								if not REShootGunEvent then
									continue
								end

								exitTo3 = 1

								break
							end
						until exitTo3 ~= 1
					end

					SoulGuitar = false
				elseif v617 then
					_tp(v617)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
