do
	local fishReplicated = replicated:WaitForChild("FishReplicated", 10)
	local fishingRequest = fishReplicated and fishReplicated:WaitForChild("FishingRequest", 10)
	local module = fishReplicated and require(fishReplicated.FishingClient.Config)
	local module2 = require(replicated.Util.GetWaterHeightAtLocation)
	local maxLaunchDistance = module and module.Rod and module.Rod.MaxLaunchDistance or 100
	local tablo6 = {
		bite = false,
		biteAt = 0,
		casting = false,
		castAt = 0,
		blockedSince = nil,
	}

	task.spawn(function()
		if not fishReplicated then
			return
		end

		local function func()
			return fishReplicated:WaitForChild("FishingRemote", 15)
		end

		local pcallOk, result = pcall(func)

		if pcallOk and result then
			result.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg, arg2)
				if arg ~= plr then
					return
				end

				if arg2 == "SpawnFishOnBob" then
					tablo6.bite = true
					tablo6.biteAt = tick()
				elseif arg2 == "ResetBiting" then
					tablo6.bite = false
				elseif arg2 == "ReeledIn" then
					tablo6.bite = false
					tablo6.casting = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	FindCastTarget = function(arg, part2, inst)
		local tablo7 = {
			arg,
			workspace.Characters,
			workspace.Enemies,
		}
		local module2_2 = module2(part2.Position)
		local value = (inst:GetAttribute("MaxLaunchDistance") or maxLaunchDistance) * 0.99751243781094523

		for _, v in ipairs({
			0,
			45,
			-45,
			90,
			-90,
			135,
			-135,
			180,
		}) do
			local cFrame3 = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(v), 0) * part2.CFrame.LookVector
			local _, findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(arg.Head.Position, cFrame3 * value), tablo7)
			local findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList2, findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList3 = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(
				Ray.new(findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), Vector3.new(0, -500, 0)),
				tablo7
			)

			if findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList2 or findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList3.Y < module2_2 then
				local vector3 = Vector3.new(findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList.X, math.max(findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList3.Y, module2_2), findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList.Z)

				if
					not (
						findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList2 and game:GetService("CollectionService"):HasTag(findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList2, module.WATER_BODY_TAG)
						or vector3.Y <= module2_2
					)
				then
					continue
				end

				part2.CFrame = CFrame.new(part2.Position, part2.Position + Vector3.new(cFrame3.X, 0, cFrame3.Z))

				return vector3
			end
		end

		local _, v667 =
			workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(arg.Head.Position, part2.CFrame.LookVector * value), tablo7)

		return Vector3.new(v667.X, math.max(v667.Y, module2_2), v667.Z)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(0.4) do
			if _G.AutoFishing then
				local exitTo

				repeat
					pcall(function()
						local humanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local humanoid2 = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoid2 or humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
							return
						end

						local tool = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
						local _GSelectedRod = tool
							and _G.SelectedRod
							and tool.Name ~= _G.SelectedRod
							and plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)

						if tool then
							if tool:FindFirstChild("FishingRodData") and not _GSelectedRod then
								if tablo6.bite or tablo6.casting and tool:GetAttribute("ServerState") == "Biting" then
									tablo6.bite = false
									fishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catching", true)
									task.wait(_G.FishCatchDelay or math.random(35, 55) / 10)
									fishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catch", 1, 0, math.random(80, 95) / 100)
									tablo6.casting = false
									task.wait(1)

									return
								end

								if tablo6.casting then
									if tick() - tablo6.castAt > 25 then
										pcall(function()
											fishingRequest:InvokeServer("ReeledIn")
										end)
										tablo6.casting = false
									end

									return
								end

								local value = Tool:GetAttribute("State")
								local value2 = Tool:GetAttribute("ServerState")

								if
									(value == nil or value == "ReeledIn")
									and (value2 == nil or value2 == "ReeledIn")
								then
									tablo6.blockedSince = nil
								else
									tablo6.blockedSince = tablo6.blockedSince or tick()

									if tick() - tablo6.blockedSince < 15 then
										return
									end
								end

								local value3 = plr.Data.FishingData:GetAttribute("SelectedBait")

								if value3 == nil or value3 == "None" then
									return
								end

								local findCastTarget = FindCastTarget(plr.Character, humanoidRootPart, Tool)

								if not findCastTarget then
									return
								end

								fishingRequest:InvokeServer("StartCasting")
								task.wait(0.2)

								local response, v678 =
									fishingRequest:InvokeServer("CastLineAtLocation", findCastTarget, 100, true)

								if response and not v678 then
									tablo6.casting = true
									tablo6.castAt = tick()
									tablo6.blockedSince = nil
								end

								return
							end
						end

						local _GSelectedRod2 = _G.SelectedRod and plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)

						if not _GSelectedRod2 then
							for _, child in ipairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Tool") and child:FindFirstChild("FishingRodData") then
									_GSelectedRod2 = child

									break
								end
							end
						end

						if _GSelectedRod2 then
							tablo6.casting = false
							humanoid:EquipTool(_GSelectedRod2)
							task.wait(0.6)
						end
					end)
					exitTo = nil

					while task.wait(0.4) do
						if _G.AutoFishing then
							exitTo = 1

							break
						end

						tablo6.casting = false
						tablo6.bite = false
					end
				until exitTo ~= 1

				return
			end

			t21.casting = false
			t21.bite = false
		end
	end)
end

HasQuest3 = function()
	local quest = plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Quest") or plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("QuestGui")

	if quest and quest:FindFirstChild("Container") then
		if quest.Container:FindFirstChild("QuestTitle") then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

	if wait(Sec) then

			pcall(function()
				if _G.TravelDres and plr.Data.Level.Value >= 700 then
					if workspace.Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide == true and workspace.Map.Ice.Door.Transparency == 0 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("DressrosaQuestProgress", "Detective")
						EquipWeapon("Key")

						while true do
							wait()
							_tp(CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488))

							if not _G.TravelDres then
								break
							elseif Root.Position == CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488).Position then
								break
							end
						end
					elseif workspace.Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide ~= false or workspace.Map.Ice.Door.Transparency ~= 1 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
					elseif Enemies:FindFirstChild("Ice Admiral") then
						local pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3 = pairs(Enemies:GetChildren())
						local geniter2 = geniter(pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3)
						local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

						if tablePack[1] then
							local tablePack2 = tablePack[3]

							while true do
								if tablePack2.Name == "Ice Admiral" and Attack.Alive(tablePack2) then
									while true do
										task.wait()
										Attack.Kill(tablePack2, _G.TravelDres)

										if _G.TravelDres == false then
											break
										elseif tablePack2.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
											break
										end
									end

									replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
								end

								local tablePack3 = table.pack(geniter2())

								if not tablePack3[1] then
									break
								end

								tablePack2 = tablePack3[3]
							end
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488))
					end
				end
			end)
		until not wait(Sec)
	end
end)

					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "2")
					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "3")
				end
			end
		end)

do
	getT = function(arg)
		local rotation

		if arg == 1 then
			rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment1.Line.Rotation
		elseif arg == 3 then
			rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment3.Line.Rotation
		elseif arg == 4 then
			rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment4.Line.Rotation
		elseif arg == 7 then
			rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment7.Line.Rotation
		elseif arg == 10 then
			rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment10.Line.Rotation
		end

		if rotation then
			return rotation.Z
		end
	end
end

do
	getRT = function(arg)
		local rotation

		for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Trophies.Quest:GetChildren()) do
			if arg == 1 and child.Name == "Trophy1" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
				rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
			elseif arg == 2 and child.Name == "Trophy2" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
				rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
			elseif arg == 3 and child.Name == "Trophy3" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
				rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
			elseif arg == 4 and child.Name == "Trophy4" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
				rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
			elseif arg == 5 and child.Name == "Trophy5" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
				rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
			end

			if rotation then
				return rotation.Z
			end
		end
	end
end

GetFirePlacard = function(arg, arg2)
	if tostring(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"]["Placard" .. arg][arg2].Indicator.BrickColor) ~= "Pearl" then
		fireclickdetector(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"]["Placard" .. arg][arg2].ClickDetector)
	end
end

										wait(2)
										_G.DoneT3 = true
									until _G.DoneT3 or _G.CDK_TS == false

									for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
										if
											(child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position - CFrame.new(
													-22695.7012,
													5270.93652,
													3814.42847,
													0.11794927,
													3.32185834e-08,
													0.99301964,
													-8.73070718e-08,
													1,
													-2.30819008e-08,
													-0.99301964,
													-8.3975138e-08,
													0.11794927
												).Position).Magnitude
												<= 300
											and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
											and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
											and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid").Health > 0
										then
											while true do
												wait()
												Attack.Kill(child, _G.CDK_TS)

												if not _G.CDK_TS then
													break
												elseif child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not child.Parent then
													break
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							else
								_tp(
									replicated:FindFirstChild("Cake Queen").HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
										* CFrame.new(0, 30, 0)
								)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	while wait(Sec) do
		if _G.Tp_LgS then
			pcall(function()
				local humanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not humanoidRootPart2 then
					return
				end

				local result2
				local magnitude2

				for _, v in ipairs({
					workspace:FindFirstChild("NPCs"),
					replicated:FindFirstChild("NPCs"),
				}) do
					if v then
						for _, child in ipairs(v:GetChildren()) do
							if string.find(child.Name, "Legendary Sword Dealer", 1, true) then
								local pcallOk, pivot = pcall(function()
									return child:GetPivot()
								end)

								if pcallOk then
									local magnitude3 = (pivot.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

									if not magnitude2 or magnitude3 < magnitude2 then
										result2 = pivot
										magnitude2 = magnitude3
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if result2 then
					_tp(result2)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

					end

						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", "Pole (2nd Form)")
					end

					if GetBP("Pole (1st Form)") and GetBP("Pole (1st Form)").Level.Value <= 179 then
						_G.Level = true
					elseif GetBP("Pole (1st Form)") and GetBP("Pole (1st Form)").Level.Value >= 180 then
						_G.Level = false
					end

					if not GetBP("Rumble Fruit") then
						return
					end

					local z = not GetBP("Rumble Fruit").AwakenedMoves:FindFirstChild("Z")

					if not z then
						z = not GetBP("Rumble Fruit").AwakenedMoves:FindFirstChild("X")
					end

					local z2 = z

					if not z2 then
						z2 = not GetBP("Rumble Fruit").AwakenedMoves:FindFirstChild("C")
					end

					local z2_2 = z2

					if not z2_2 then
						z2_2 = not GetBP("Rumble Fruit").AwakenedMoves:FindFirstChild("V")
					end

					local z2_2_2 = z2_2

					if not z2_2_2 then
						z2_2_2 = not GetBP("Rumble Fruit").AwakenedMoves:FindFirstChild("F")
					end

					if z2_2_2 then
						local ok = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Awakener", "Check") == nil

						if not ok then
							ok = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Awakener", "Check") == 0
						end

						if ok then
							_G.SelectChip = "Rumble"

							local invokeServer = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaidsNpc", "Select", _G.SelectChip)

							if invokeServer then
								invokeServer:Stop()
							end

							_G.Raiding = true
							_G.Auto_Awakener = true
						end
					else
						_G.SelectChip = nil
						_G.Raiding = false
						_G.Auto_Awakener = false

						if plr.Data.Fragments.Value >= 5000 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Thunder God", "Talk")
							wait(Sec)
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Thunder God", "Sure")
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		until not wait(Sec)
	end

		while wait(0.1) do
			pcall(function()
				if _G.LongsWord then
					local getConnectionEnemies = GetConnectionEnemies("Diamond")

					if getConnectionEnemies then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							Attack.Kill(getConnectionEnemies, _G.LongsWord)

							if _G.LongsWord == false then
								break
							elseif not getConnectionEnemies.Parent or getConnectionEnemies.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
								break
							end
						end

						return
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(-1576.7166748047, 198.59265136719, 13.724286079407))
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

spawn(function()
	while wait(Sec) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.DarkBladev3 and World2 then
				if not GetBP("Dark Blade") then
					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", "Dark Blade")
				end

				if not GetBP("Fist of Darkness") then
					_G.AutoFarmChest = true
				elseif workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Darkbeard") then
					if GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard") and GetBP("Fist of Darkness") >= 1 then
						while true do
							wait()
							_tp(CFrame.new(-5719.36376953125, 48.505905151367188, -782.9759521484375))

							if not _G.DarkBladev3 then
								break
							elseif
								Root.Position
								== CFrame.new(-5719.36376953125, 48.505905151367188, -782.9759521484375).Position
							then
								break
							end
						end

						fireclickdetector(workspace.Map.GraveIsland.Mountain.Rocks.Button.ClickDetector)
					end
				else
					_tp(CFrame.new(3677.08203125, 62.751937866211, -3144.8332519531))
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

							)

					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.Auto_Def_DarkCoat then
			local exitTo

			repeat
				pcall(function()
					local getBP = not GetBP("Fist of Darkness")

					if not getBP then
						getBP = workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Darkbeard")
					end

					if not getBP then
						_tp(CFrame.new(3677.08203125, 62.751937866211, -3144.8332519531))
					elseif GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard") then
						local getConnectionEnemies = GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard")

						if getConnectionEnemies then
							while true do
								wait()
								Attack.Kill(getConnectionEnemies, _G.Auto_Def_DarkCoat)

								if _G.Auto_Def_DarkCoat == false then
									break
								elseif not getConnectionEnemies.Parent or getConnectionEnemies.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					elseif not (GetBP("Fist of Darkness") or GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard")) then
						while true do
							wait(0.1)
							_G.AutoFarmChest = true

							if not _G.Auto_Def_DarkCoat then
								break
							elseif GetBP("Fist of Darkness") or GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard") then
								break
							end
						end

						_G.AutoFarmChest = false
					end
				end)
				exitTo = nil

				while wait(0.1) do
					if _G.Auto_Def_DarkCoat then
						exitTo = 1

						break
					end
				end
			until exitTo ~= 1

			return
		end
	end
end)

do
	spawn(function()
		while wait(0.1) do
			if _G.Auto_DonAcces then
				pcall(function()
					if
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil
						and plr.Data.Level.Value >= 1500
					then
						FruitPrice = {}
						FruitStore = {}

						local next = next
						local invokeServer, invokeServer2 = replicated:WaitForChild("Remotes").CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")

						for _, v in next, invokeServer, invokeServer2 do
							if v.Price >= 1000000 then
								table.insert(FruitPrice, v.Name)
							end
						end

						for _, v in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventoryFruits")) do
							for k, value in pairs(v) do
								if k == "Name" then
									table.insert(FruitStore, value)
								end
							end

							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Cousin", "Buy")

							for _, value in pairs(FruitPrice) do
								for _, value2 in pairs(FruitStore) do
									if value == value2 then
										if
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess
											== nil
										then
											_G.StoreF = false

											if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(FruitStore) then
												replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor", "1")
												replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor", "2")
												replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor", "3")
											else
												replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadFruit", tostring(value))
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end

							if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess ~= nil then
								_G.StoreF = true
								_G.Auto_DonAcces = false
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

						end

						if GetBP("Black Leg") and GetBP("Black Leg").Level.Value >= 400 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDeathStep")
							_G.Level = false
						elseif GetBP("Black Leg") and GetBP("Black Leg").Level.Value < 399 then
							_G.Level = true
						end

						if
							(GetBP("Black Leg") or GetBP("Black Leg").Level.Value >= 400)
							and workspace.Map.IceCastle.Hall.LibraryDoor.PhoeyuDoor.Transparency == 0
						then
							if GetBP("Library Key") then
								while true do
									wait()
									_tp(CFrame.new(6371.2001953125, 296.63433837890625, -6841.18115234375))

									if not _G.AutoDeathStep then
										break
									elseif
										Root.Position
										== CFrame.new(6371.2001953125, 296.63433837890625, -6841.18115234375).Position
									then
										break
									end
								end

								if
									Root.CFrame == CFrame.new(6371.2001953125, 296.63433837890625, -6841.18115234375)
								then
									replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDeathStep")
								end
							elseif not GetBP("Library Key") then
								local getConnectionEnemies = GetConnectionEnemies("Awakened Ice Admiral")

								if getConnectionEnemies then
									while true do
										wait()
										Attack.Kill(getConnectionEnemies, _G.AutoDeathStep)

										if not getConnectionEnemies.Parent then
											break
										elseif
											getConnectionEnemies.Humanoid.Health <= 0
											or _G.AutoDeathStep == false
											or GetBP("Library Key")
											or GetBP("Death Step")
										then
											break
										end
									end
								else
									_tp(CFrame.new(5668.9780273438, 28.519989013672, -6483.3520507813))
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				exitTo = nil

				while wait(Sec) do
					if _G.AutoDeathStep then
						exitTo = 1

						break
					end
				end
			until exitTo ~= 1

			return
		end
	end
end)

do
	spawn(function()
		while wait(0.2) do
			if _G.FarmChestM then
				pcall(function()
					local diamondChest = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland").Chests:FindFirstChild("DiamondChest")

					if not diamondChest then
						diamondChest = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland").Chests:FindFirstChild("FragChest")
					end

					if diamondChest then
						local collectionService2 = game:GetService("CollectionService")
						local localPlayer3 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
						local character2 = localPlayer3.Character or localPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Wait()

						if not character2 then
							return
						end

						local position = character2:GetPivot().Position
						local tagged = collectionService2:GetTagged("_ChestTagged")
						local mathHuge = math.huge
						local v918

						for i = 1, #tagged do
							local taggedInstance = tagged[i]
							local magnitude2 = (taggedInstance:GetPivot().Position - position).Magnitude

							if
								(not SelectedIsland or taggedInstance:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland))
								and not taggedInstance:GetAttribute("IsDisabled")
								and magnitude2 < mathHuge
							then
								mathHuge = magnitude2
								v918 = taggedInstance
							end
						end

						if v918 then
							_tp(v918:GetPivot())
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

MoveCamtoMoon = function()
	workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
		workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position,
		Lighting:GetMoonDirection() + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
	)
	plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(
		plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position,
		Lighting:GetMoonDirection() + plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position
	)
end
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if LookM then
			MoveCamtoMoon()
			wait(0.1)
			replicated.Remotes.CommE:FireServer("ActivateAbility")
		end
	end
end)

do
	MoveCamtoMoon = function()
		local getMoonDirection = Lighting:GetMoonDirection()

		workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame =
			CFrame.new(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + getMoonDirection)
		plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame =
			CFrame.new(plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + getMoonDirection)
	end
end

			userInputService:SendKeyEvent(false, "T", false, game)
		end
	end

				elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Wenlocktoad", "1") == 0 then
					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Wenlocktoad", "2")

							wait()
							Attack.Kill(getConnectionEnemies, _G.Auto_Human)

							if getConnectionEnemies.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
								break
							elseif not getConnectionEnemies.Parent or not _G.Auto_Human then
								break
							end
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(-2172.7399902344, 103.32216644287, -4015.025390625))
					end

					local getConnectionEnemies2 = GetConnectionEnemies(Human_v3_Mob[2])

							end
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(2006.9261474609, 448.95666503906, 853.98284912109))
					end

					local getConnectionEnemies3 = GetConnectionEnemies(Human_v3_Mob[3])

do
	GetSeaBeastTrial = function()
		if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("FishmanTrial") then
			return nil
		end

		if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water") then
			FishmanTrial = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water")
		end

		if FishmanTrial then
			local next = next
			local children, children2 = workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

			for _, v in next, children, children2 do
				if
					v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					and (v.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FishmanTrial.Position).Magnitude <= 1500
					and v.Health.Value > 0
				then
					return v
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

	while wait(Sec) do

							and (Root.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 250

								_tp(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 15))
								sethiddenproperty(plr, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)

								if _G.Defeating == false then
									break
								elseif
									child.Humanoid.Health <= 0
									or not child.Parent
									or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									or not child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
								then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

spawn(function()
	while wait(Sec) do
		if _G.Dojoo then
			pcall(function()
				local tablo6 = { {
					NPC = "Dojo Trainer",
					Command = "RequestQuest",
				} }
				local net =
					replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest"):InvokeServer(unpack(tablo6))
				local printBeltName2 = printBeltName(net)

				if not (debug ~= false or net or printBeltName2) then
					_tp(CFrame.new(5865.0234375, 1208.3154296875, 871.15185546875))
					debug = true
				elseif
					debug == true
					and (
							CFrame.new(5865.0234375, 1208.3154296875, 871.15185546875).Position
							- plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
						).Magnitude
						<= 50
				then
					if printBeltName2 == "White" then
						local getConnectionEnemies = GetConnectionEnemies("Skull Slayer")

						if getConnectionEnemies then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.Kill(getConnectionEnemies, _G.Dojoo)
								net = replicated.Modules.Net
									:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest")
									:InvokeServer(unpack(tablo6))

								if not net then
									break
								elseif not _G.Dojoo or not Attack.Alive(getConnectionEnemies) then
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-16759.58984375, 71.283767700195312, 1595.3399658203125))
						end
					elseif printBeltName2 == "Yellow" then
						while true do
							task.wait(1)
							_G.SeaBeast1 = true
							_G.TerrorShark = true
							_G.Shark = true
							_G.Piranha = true

								:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest")
								:InvokeServer(unpack(tablo6))

							if not _G.Dojoo then
								break
							elseif not net then
								break

						end

						_G.FarmEliteHunt = false

											:InvokeServer(unpack(tablo6))

										end

									_G.Prehis_Skills = true
									_G.Prehis_Find = false

									end

					elseif printBeltName2 == "Orange" or printBeltName2 == "Blue" then
						return nil
					end
				end

				if not response then
					debug = false

						{
							NPC = "Dojo Trainer",
							Command = "ClaimQuest",
						},
					}))
				end
			end)
		end
	end

checkQuesta = function()
	local tablo6 = { {
		Context = "Check",
	} }
	local replicatedStorage2

	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			:WaitForChild("Modules")
			:WaitForChild("Net")
			:WaitForChild("RF/DragonHunter")
			:InvokeServer(unpack({ {
				Context = "RequestQuest",
			} }))
	end)

	local _ = pcall(function()
		replicatedStorage2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			:WaitForChild("Modules")
			:WaitForChild("Net")
			:WaitForChild("RF/DragonHunter")
			:InvokeServer(unpack(tablo6))
	end)
	local v962 = false
	local toNumber
	local v2
	local n8

	if replicatedStorage2 and replicatedStorage2.Text then
		v962 = true

		local text = replicatedStorage2.Text

		if string.find(tostring(text), "Defeat") then
			n8 = 1
			toNumber = tonumber(string.sub(tostring(text), 8, 9))

			for _, v in pairs({
				"Hydra Enforcer",
				"Venomous Assailant",
			}) do
				if string.find(text, v) then
					v2 = v

					break
				end
			end
		elseif string.find(tostring(text), "Destroy") then
			toNumber = 10
			n8 = 2
			v2 = nil
		end
	end

	return v962, v2, toNumber, n8
end
BackTODoJo = function()
	for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren()) do
		if
			child.Name == "NotificationTemplate"
			and string.find(child.Text, "Head back to the Dojo to complete more tasks")
		then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end
DragonMobClear = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	if workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(arg2) then
		for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == arg2 and Attack.Alive(child) and arg then
				Attack.Kill(child, arg)
			end
		end
	else
		_tp(arg3)
	end
end

			{
				NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
				Command = "Upgrade",
			},
		}))
	end
end

				end

					local geniter2 = geniter(pairsIterator, pairsIterator2, pairsIterator3)
					local exitTo

					while true do
						local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

						if not tablePack[1] then
							break
						end

							position = tablePack2.PrimaryPart.Position

								(position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
								<= 100

								exitTo = 1

								break
							end
						end
					end

					if exitTo == 1 then
						local exitTo2

						repeat
							_tp(CFrame.new(position))
							exitTo2 = nil

							while true do
								local tablePack = table.pack(v983())

								if not tablePack[1] then
									break
								end

								end

					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

spawn(function()
	while wait(Sec) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Prehis_Find then
				local character2 = plr.Character

				if not character2 then
					return
				end

				local humanoidRootPart2 = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local humanoid2 = character2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoid2 or humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end

				local prehistoricIsland = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Prehistoric Island", true)

				if prehistoricIsland then
					local headTeleport = prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("HeadTeleport", true)
						or prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Teleport_Head", true)
						or prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Head", true)

					if headTeleport then
						local cFrame3 = headTeleport.CFrame
						local position = cFrame3.Position - cFrame3.LookVector * 40 + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)

						if (position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
							_tp(CFrame.new(position))
						end
					else
						local position = prehistoricIsland.CFrame.Position

						_tp(
							CFrame.new(
								position - (position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Unit * 250 + Vector3.new(0, 60, 0)
							)
						)
					end
				else
					local checkBoat = CheckBoat()

					if not checkBoat then
						local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

						TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

						if (cFrame3.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat or "Guardian")
						end

						return
					end

					if humanoid2.Sit == false then
						_tp(checkBoat.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))

						return
					end

					local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)

					targetDestination = cFrame3

					if CheckEnemiesBoat() then
						_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))
					else
						if CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
							_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))

							return
						end

						_tp(cFrame3)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait(0.3) do
		if _G.Prehis_Skills then
			pcall(function()
				local prehistoricIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")

				if not prehistoricIsland then
					return
				end

				for _, descendant in pairs(prehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
						if descendant.Name:lower():find("lava") then
							descendant:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end

				local core = PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Core")

				if core then
					local interiorLava = core:FindFirstChild("InteriorLava")

					if interiorLava then
						interiorLava:Destroy()
					end
				end

				local trialTeleport = PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("TrialTeleport")

				for _, descendant in pairs(PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
					if
						descendant.Name == "TouchInterest"
						and (not trialTeleport or not descendant:IsDescendantOf(trialTeleport))
					then
						descendant.Parent:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

					if glow and glow.Enabled then
						repeat
							wait(0.1)

							if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(vFXLayer.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then

								wait(0.4)
								Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")

						until not _G.Prehis_Skills or not glow.Enabled
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

				if not trialTeleport or not trialTeleport:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
					if workspace:FindFirstChild("DinoBone") then
						for _, child in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "DinoBone" then
								_tp(child.CFrame)
							end
						end
					end
				elseif tick() - (getgenv().LastPHReset or 0) > 10 then
					getgenv().LastPHReset = tick()
					plr.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

spawn(function()

do
	spawn(function()
		while wait() do
			pcall(function()
				for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Boats:GetChildren()) do
					for _, descendant in pairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							if getgenv().NoClipShip or getgenv().FindPrehistoric then
								descendant.CanCollide = false
							else
								descendant.CanCollide = true
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.SailBoats then
			pcall(function()
				local checkBoat = CheckBoat()

				if
					not checkBoat
					and (not CheckShark() or not _G.Shark)
					and (not CheckTerrorShark() or not _G.TerrorShark)
					and (not CheckFishCrew() or not _G.MobCrew)
					and (not CheckPiranha() or not _G.Piranha)
					and (not CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.FishBoat)
					and (not CheckSeaBeast() or not _G.SeaBeast1)
					and (not _G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade())
					and (not _G.HCM or not CheckHauntedCrew())
					and (not _G.Leviathan1 or not CheckLeviathan())
				then
					local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

					TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

					if (cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat)
					end
				elseif
					checkBoat
					and (not CheckShark() or not _G.Shark)
					and (not CheckTerrorShark() or not _G.TerrorShark)
					and (not CheckFishCrew() or not _G.MobCrew)
				then
					if
						(not CheckPiranha() or not _G.Piranha)
						and (not CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.FishBoat)
						and (not CheckSeaBeast() or not _G.SeaBeast1)
						and (not _G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade())
						and (not _G.HCM or not CheckHauntedCrew())
						and (not _G.Leviathan1 or not CheckLeviathan())
					then
						if plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
							_tp(checkBoat.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))
						else
							if _G.DangerSc == "Lv 1" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-21998.375, 30.0006084, -682.309143)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 2" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-26779.5215, 30.0005474, -822.858032)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 3" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-31171.957, 30.0001011, -2256.93774)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 4" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-34054.6875, 30.2187767, -2560.12012)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 5" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-38887.5547, 30.0004578, -2162.99023)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 6" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-44541.7617, 30.0003204, -1244.8584)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv Infinite" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)
							end

							while true do
								wait()

								if
									(_G.FishBoat or not CheckEnemiesBoat())
									and (_G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade())
									and (_G.TerrorShark or not CheckTerrorShark())
								then
									_tp(CFrameSelectedZone)
								else
									_tp(CFrameSelectedZone * CFrame.new(0, 150, 0))
								end

								if
									_G.SailBoats ~= false
									and (not CheckShark() or not _G.Shark)
									and (not CheckTerrorShark() or not _G.TerrorShark)
									and (not CheckFishCrew() or not _G.MobCrew)
									and (not CheckPiranha() or not _G.Piranha)
									and (not CheckSeaBeast() or not _G.SeaBeast1)
									and (not CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.FishBoat)
									and (not _G.Leviathan1 or not CheckLeviathan())
									and (not _G.HCM or not CheckHauntedCrew())
									and (not _G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade())
								then
									if plr.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Sit ~= false then
										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end

							plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

	while wait(Sec) do

							descendant.CanCollide = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Shark then
				local tablo6 = { "Shark" }

				if CheckShark() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(tablo6, child.Name) and Attack.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.Kill(child, _G.Shark)

								if _G.Shark == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.TerrorShark then
				local tablo6 = { "Terrorshark" }

				if CheckTerrorShark() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(tablo6, child.Name) and Attack.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.KillSea(child, _G.TerrorShark)

								if _G.TerrorShark == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.Piranha then
				local tablo6 = { "Piranha" }

				if CheckPiranha() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(tablo6, child.Name) and Attack.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.Kill(child, _G.Piranha)

								if _G.Piranha == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.MobCrew then
				local tablo6 = { "Fish Crew Member" }

				if CheckFishCrew() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(tablo6, child.Name) and Attack.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.Kill(child, _G.MobCrew)

								if _G.MobCrew == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.HCM then
				local tablo6 = { "Haunted Crew Member" }

				if CheckHauntedCrew() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(tablo6, child.Name) and Attack.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								Attack.Kill(child, _G.HCM)

								if _G.HCM == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.SeaBeast1 then
				if workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild("SeaBeast1") then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
						if
							child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							and child:FindFirstChild("Health")
							and child.Health.Value > 0
						then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								spawn(function()
									_tp(
										CFrame.new(
											child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X,
											game:GetService("Workspace").Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200,
											child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z
										)
									)
								end)

								if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position) <= 500 then
									AitSeaSkill_Custom = child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
									MousePos = AitSeaSkill_Custom.Position

									if CheckF() then
										weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									else
										Useskills("Melee", "Z")
										Useskills("Melee", "X")
										Useskills("Melee", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Sword", "Z")
										Useskills("Sword", "X")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Gun", "Z")
										Useskills("Gun", "X")
									end
								end
							until _G.SeaBeast1 == false
								or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								or not child.Parent
								or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.Leviathan1 then
				if workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild("Leviathan") then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
						if
							child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							and child:FindFirstChild("Leviathan Segment")
							and child:FindFirstChild("Health")
							and child.Health.Value > 0
						then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								spawn(function()
									_tp(
										CFrame.new(
											child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X,
											game:GetService("Workspace").Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200,
											child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z
										)
									)
								end)

								if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position) <= 500 then
									MousePos = child:FindFirstChild("Leviathan Segment").Position

									if CheckF() then
										weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									else
										Useskills("Melee", "Z")
										Useskills("Melee", "X")
										Useskills("Melee", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Sword", "Z")
										Useskills("Sword", "X")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Gun", "Z")
										Useskills("Gun", "X")
									end
								end
							until _G.Leviathan1 == false
								or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								or not child.Parent
								or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.FishBoat and CheckEnemiesBoat() then
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
					if
						child:FindFirstChild("Health")
						and child.Health.Value > 0
						and child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
					then
						repeat
							task.wait()
							spawn(function()
								if child.Name == "FishBoat" then
									_tp(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -50, -25))
								end
							end)

							if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.Engine.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then
								AitSeaSkill_Custom = child.Engine.CFrame
								MousePos = AitSeaSkill_Custom.Position

								if CheckF() then
									weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
								else
									Useskills("Melee", "Z")
									Useskills("Melee", "X")
									Useskills("Melee", "C")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Sword", "Z")
									Useskills("Sword", "X")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Gun", "Z")
									Useskills("Gun", "X")
								end
							end
						until _G.FishBoat == false or not child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") or child.Health.Value <= 0
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.PGB and CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
					if
						child:FindFirstChild("Health")
						and child.Health.Value > 0
						and child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
					then
						repeat
							task.wait()
							spawn(function()
								if child.Name == "PirateBrigade" then
									_tp(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -30, -10))
								elseif child.Name == "PirateGrandBrigade" then
									_tp(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -50, -50))
								end
							end)

							if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.Engine.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then
								AitSeaSkill_Custom = child.Engine.CFrame
								MousePos = AitSeaSkill_Custom.Position

								if CheckF() then
									weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
								else
									Useskills("Melee", "Z")
									Useskills("Melee", "X")
									Useskills("Melee", "C")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Sword", "Z")
									Useskills("Sword", "X")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Gun", "Z")
									Useskills("Gun", "X")
								end
							end

					end

		end)

	spawn(function()

				pcall(function()
					if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island", true) then
						_tp(
							workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island").CFrame
								* CFrame.new(0, 500, 0)
						)
					else
						local checkBoat = CheckBoat()

						if checkBoat then
							if plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
								_tp(checkBoat.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))
							else
								local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)

								while true do
									wait()

									if CheckEnemiesBoat() or CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
										_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))
									else
										_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25))
									end

									if
										_G.AutofindKitIs
										and not (
											(cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10
										)
									then
										if
											not (
												workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island")
												or plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false
											)
										then
											continue
										end
									end

									break
								end

								plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
							end
						else
							local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

							TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

							if (cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
								replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat)
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.tweenShrine then
			pcall(function()
				local kitsuneIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland")
					or game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island")
				local shrineActive = kitsuneIsland:FindFirstChild("ShrineActive")

				if shrineActive then
					local next = next
					local descendants, descendants2 = shrineActive:GetDescendants()

					for _, v in next, descendants, descendants2 do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") then
							if v.Name:find("NeonShrinePart") then
								replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RE/TouchKitsuneStatue"):FireServer()

								while true do
									wait()
									_tp(v.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0))

									if _G.tweenShrine == false then
										break
									elseif not kitsuneIsland then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				else
					_tp(
						workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island").CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 0)
					)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.Collect_Ember then
			pcall(function()
				if workspace:WaitForChild("AttachedAzureEmber") then
					notween(workspace:WaitForChild("EmberTemplate"):FindFirstChild("Part").CFrame)
				elseif workspace:WaitForChild("EmberTemplate") then

					return
				else

				end
			end)
		end
	end

spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.FrozenTP then
			local exitTo

			repeat
				pcall(function()
					if workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("LeviathanGate") then
						_tp(workspace.Map.LeviathanGate.CFrame)
						replicated:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("CommF_"):InvokeServer("OpenLeviathanGate")
					end
				end)
				exitTo = nil

				while wait(0.1) do
					if _G.FrozenTP then
						exitTo = 1

						break
					end
				end
			until exitTo ~= 1

			return
		end
	end
end)

									_tp(CFrame.new(5433, 150, 290))

						TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

						if (cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat)
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)

do
	isnil = function(arg)
		return arg == nil
	end
end

do
	round = function(arg)
		return math.floor(tonumber(arg) + 0.5)
	end
end

do
	formatNumber = function(arg)
		local toString = tostring(arg)
		local gsub

		repeat
			toString, gsub = toString:gsub("^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
		until gsub == 0

		return toString
	end
end

do
	getFruitStock = function()
		local s4 = "Advance Fruit Stock\n"
		local pcallOk, invokeServer = pcall(function()
			return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits", true)
		end)

		if pcallOk and invokeServer then
			local v1091 = false

			for _, v in pairs(invokeServer) do
				if v.OnSale then
					v1091 = true
					s4 ..= v.Name .. " - $" .. formatNumber(v.Price) .. "\n"
				end
			end

			if not v1091 then
				s4 ..= "- No fruit.\n"
			end
		else
			s4 ..= "- Error while retrieving data.\n"
		end

		local s4_2 = s4 .. "\nNormal Fruit Stock\n"
		local pcallOk2, invokeServer2 = pcall(function()
			return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")
		end)

		if pcallOk2 and invokeServer2 then
			local v1097 = false

			for _, v in pairs(invokeServer2) do
				if v.OnSale then
					v1097 = true
					s4_2 ..= v.Name .. " - $" .. formatNumber(v.Price) .. "\n"
				end
			end

			if not v1097 then
				s4_2 ..= "- No fruit.\n"
			end
		else
			s4_2 ..= "- Error while retrieving data.\n"
		end

		return s4_2
	end
end

spawn(function()
	while wait(5) do
		pcall(function()
			if not _G.Random_Auto then
				return
			end

			local spinnerWindow = plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpinnerWindow")

			if spinnerWindow then
				local aboveSpinner = spinnerWindow:FindFirstChild("AboveSpinner")
					and spinnerWindow.AboveSpinner:FindFirstChild("Navigation")
					and spinnerWindow.AboveSpinner.Navigation:FindFirstChild("CloseButton")

				if aboveSpinner and aboveSpinner.Visible then
					pcall(function()
						for _, v in pairs(getconnections(aboveSpinner.Activated)) do
							if v.Function then
								v.Function()
							elseif v.Fire then
								v:Fire()
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end

			if plr.Data.Level.Value < 50 then
				return
			end

			if RandomFruitCooldown and tick() < RandomFruitCooldown then
				return
			end

			local module = require(replicated.Controllers.GachaClient)
			local pcallOk, purchaseGachaAsync, result2 = pcall(function()
				return module.PurchaseGachaAsync("ZiolesGacha")
			end)

			if pcallOk and type(result2) == "table" then
				if result2.Cooldown and result2.Cooldown.TimeEnds then
					local timeEnds = result2.Cooldown.TimeEnds - os.time()

					if timeEnds > 0 then
						RandomFruitCooldown = tick() + timeEnds

						return
					end
				end

				if result2.RequirementsMet == false then
					RandomFruitCooldown = tick() + 600

					return
				end

				if purchaseGachaAsync == true then
					RandomFruitCooldown = tick() + 7200
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

IsRaidActive = function()
	local pcallOk, result = pcall(function()
		if plr.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
			return true
		end

		local value = plr:GetAttribute("IslandRaiding")

		return value ~= nil and value ~= false and value ~= ""
	end)
	local pcallOk2 = pcallOk and result == true

	return pcallOk2
end

					end

					end

					if not cFrame3 then
						return
					end

					if not humanoidRootPart2 then
						return
					end

						_tp(cFrame3)

						return
					end

						fireclickdetector(workspace.Map.CircleIsland.RaidSummon2.Button.Main.ClickDetector)
					else

							fireclickdetector(main.ClickDetector)
						end
					end

					tick2 = tick()
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

ListServers = function(arg)
	local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local games = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"
		.. tostring(game.PlaceId)
		.. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
		.. (arg and "&cursor=" .. arg or "")
	local pcallOk, response = pcall(function()
		return game:HttpGet(games)
	end)

	if pcallOk and response then
		local pcallOk2, decoded = pcall(function()
			return httpService:JSONDecode(response)
		end)

		if pcallOk2 then
			return decoded
		end
	end

	return {
		data = {},
	}
end
fetchServersData = function(arg, arg2)
	local public = string.format("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?limit=%d", arg, arg2 or 100)
	local pcallOk, decoded = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(public))
	end)

	if not pcallOk or not decoded or not decoded.data then
		return nil
	end

	return decoded.data
end
IsEntityAlive = function(character2)
	if not character2 then
		return false
	end

	local humanoid2 = character2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	return humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health > 0
end
GetEnemiesInRange = function(arg, arg2)
	local tablo6 = {}
	local position = arg:GetPivot().Position
	local playerList = game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()
	local children = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()

	for _, child in ipairs(children) do
		local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if humanoidRootPart2 and IsEntityAlive(child) and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude <= arg2 then
			table.insert(tablo6, child)
		end
	end

	for _, player in ipairs(playerList) do
		if player ~= plr and player.Character then
			local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if
				humanoidRootPart2
				and IsEntityAlive(player.Character)
				and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude <= arg2
			then
				table.insert(tablo6, player.Character)
			end
		end
	end

	return tablo6
end

do
	AttackNoCoolDown = function()
		if not plr.Character then
			return
		end

		local v1196
		local iPairsIterator, iPairsIterator2, iPairsIterator3 = ipairs(plr.Character:GetChildren())
		local geniter2 = geniter(iPairsIterator, iPairsIterator2, iPairsIterator3)

		while true do
			local tablePack = table.pack(geniter2())

			if not tablePack[1] then
				break
			end

			local tablePack2 = tablePack[3]

			if tablePack2:IsA("Tool") then
				v1196 = tablePack2

				break
			end
		end

		if not v1190 then
			return
		end

		local getEnemiesInRange = GetEnemiesInRange(plr.Character, 60)

		if #getEnemiesInRange == 0 then
			return
		end

		local replicatedStorage2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local modules = replicatedStorage2:FindFirstChild("Modules")

		if not modules then
			return
		end

		local RERegisterAttack =
			replicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RE/RegisterAttack")
		local RERegisterHit =
			replicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RE/RegisterHit")

		if not RERegisterAttack or not RERegisterHit then
			return
		end

		local tablo6 = {}
		local primaryPart
		local iPairsIterator, iPairsIterator2, iPairsIterator3 = ipairs(getEnemiesInRange)

		for _, v in iPairsIterator, iPairsIterator2, iPairsIterator3 do
			if not v:GetAttribute("IsBoat") then
				local tablo7 = {
					"RightLowerArm",
					"RightUpperArm",
					"LeftLowerArm",
					"LeftUpperArm",
					"RightHand",
					"LeftHand",
				}
				local child = v:FindFirstChild(tablo7[math.random(#tablo7)]) or v.PrimaryPart

				if child then
					table.insert(tablo6, {
						v,
						child,
					})
					primaryPart = child
				end
			end
		end

		if not primaryPart then
			return
		end

		RERegisterAttack:FireServer(0)

		local playerScripts = plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")

		if not playerScripts then
			return
		end

		local localScript = playerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass("LocalScript")

		while not localScript do
			playerScripts.ChildAdded:Wait()
			localScript = playerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass("LocalScript")
		end

		local sendHitsToServer

		if getsenv then
			local pcallOk, result = pcall(getsenv, localScript)

			if pcallOk and result then
				sendHitsToServer = result._G.SendHitsToServer
			end
		end

		local pcallOk, cOMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD = pcall(function()
			return require(modules.Flags).COMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD or false
		end)

		if pcallOk and cOMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD and sendHitsToServer then
			sendHitsToServer(primaryPart, tablo6)
		elseif pcallOk and not cOMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD then
			RERegisterHit:FireServer(primaryPart, tablo6)
		end
	end
end

CameraShakerR = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Util.CameraShaker)
CameraShakerR:Stop()
get_Monster = function()
	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
		local upperTorso = child:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or child:FindFirstChild("Head")

		if
			child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true)
			and upperTorso
			and (child.Head.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 50
		then
			return true, upperTorso.Position
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
		if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
			return true, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
		if not child:FindFirstChild("Health") or not (child.Health.Value > 0) then
			continue
		end

		if child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
			return true, child.Engine.Position
		end
	end
end
Actived = function()
	local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	local getConnections, getConnections2 = getconnections(tool.Activated)

	for _, v in next, getConnections, getConnections2 do
		if typeof(v.Function) == "function" then
			getupvalues(v.Function)
		end
	end
end

do
	task.spawn(function()
		RunSer.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				if not _G.Seriality then
					return
				end

				AttackNoCoolDown()

				local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
				local toolTip = tool.ToolTip
				local get_Monster2 = get_Monster()

				if toolTip == "Blox Fruit" and get_Monster2 then
					local leftClickRemote = tool:FindFirstChild("LeftClickRemote")

					if leftClickRemote then
						Actived()
						leftClickRemote:FireServer(Vector3.new(0.01, -500, 0.01), 1, true)
						leftClickRemote:FireServer(false)
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
	end)
end

SafeWaitForChild = function(inst, arg)
	local _, child = pcall(function()
		return inst:WaitForChild(arg)
	end)

	return child
end

local safeWaitForChild = SafeWaitForChild(workspace, "Enemies")
local safeWaitForChild2 = SafeWaitForChild(workspace, "Characters")
local safeWaitForChild3 = SafeWaitForChild(replicatedStorage2, "Modules")
local safeWaitForChild4 = SafeWaitForChild(safeWaitForChild3, "Net")
local tablo6 = {
	Rate = 2e-09,
	Enabled = true,
	IsAlive = function(character2)
		local humanoid2 = character2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

		if humanoid2 then
			if humanoid2.Health > 0 then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end,
	GetNearbyTargets = function(arg, inst)
		local tablo7 = {}
		local position = arg:GetPivot().Position
		local children = inst:GetChildren()

		for i = 1, #children do
			local child = children[i]
			local humanoid2 = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if
				humanoid2
				and humanoidRootPart2
				and humanoid2.Health > 0
				and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude <= 60
			then
				table.insert(tablo7, child)
			end
		end

		return tablo7
	end,
	GetTargetParts = function(arg)
		local tablo7 = {}

		for i = 1, #arg do
			local arg2 = arg[i]
			local head = arg2:FindFirstChild("Head") or arg2.PrimaryPart

			if head then
				table.insert(tablo7, {
					arg2,
					head,
				})
			end
		end

		return tablo7
	end,
}

tablo6.GetAllTargets = function(arg)
	local tablo7 = {}
	local getNearbyTargets = tablo6.GetNearbyTargets(arg, safeWaitForChild)
	local getNearbyTargets2 = tablo6.GetNearbyTargets(arg, safeWaitForChild2)

	for i = 1, #getNearbyTargets do
		table.insert(tablo7, getNearbyTargets[i])
	end

	for i = 1, #getNearbyTargets2 do
		table.insert(tablo7, getNearbyTargets2[i])
	end

	return tablo7
end
tablo6.ExecuteFastAttack = function()
	local character2 = localPlayer3.Character

	if not character2 then
		return
	end

	if not character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
		return
	end

	local getAllTargets = tablo6.GetAllTargets(character2)

	if #getAllTargets < 1 then
		return
	end

	local getTargetParts = tablo6.GetTargetParts(getAllTargets)

	if #getTargetParts < 1 then
		return
	end

	local safeWaitForChild4_2 = safeWaitForChild4["RE/RegisterHit"]

	safeWaitForChild4["RE/RegisterAttack"]:FireServer(tablo6.Rate)
	safeWaitForChild4_2:FireServer(getTargetParts[1][2], getTargetParts)
end

local child
local value

InitializeHitRegistration = function()
	for _, v in ipairs({
		replicatedStorage2.Util,
		replicatedStorage2.Common,
		replicatedStorage2.Remotes,
		replicatedStorage2.Assets,
		replicatedStorage2.FX,
	}) do
		local children = v:GetChildren()

		for _, child in ipairs(children) do
			if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") and child:GetAttribute("Id") then
				child = child
				value = child:GetAttribute("Id")
			end
		end

		v.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") and child:GetAttribute("Id") then
				child = child
				value = child:GetAttribute("Id")
			end
		end)
	end
end
InitializeHitRegistration()

local tablo7 = {
	Execute = function()
		local character2 = localPlayer3.Character

		if not character2 then
			return
		end

		local humanoidRootPart2 = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not humanoidRootPart2 then
			return
		end

		local tablo8 = {}

		ScanFolder = function(inst)
			local children = inst:GetChildren()

			for i = 1, #children do
				local child2 = children[i]
				local humanoid2 = child2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local humanoidRootPart3 = child2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if
					humanoid2
					and humanoidRootPart3
					and humanoid2.Health > 0
					and child2 ~= character2
					and (humanoidRootPart3.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude <= 60
				then
					local children2 = child2:GetChildren()

					for _, v in ipairs(children2) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") then
							table.insert(tablo8, {
								child2,
								v,
							})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		ScanFolder(safeWaitForChild)
		ScanFolder(safeWaitForChild2)

		local tool = character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

		if
			#tablo8 > 0
			and tool
			and (tool:GetAttribute("WeaponType") == "Melee" or tool:GetAttribute("WeaponType") == "Sword")
		then
			local invokeServer = safeWaitForChild3.Net.seed:InvokeServer()
			local safeWaitForChild4_2 = safeWaitForChild4["RE/RegisterHit"]

			safeWaitForChild4["RE/RegisterAttack"]:FireServer()

			local head = tablo8[1][1]:FindFirstChild("Head")

			if not head then
				return
			end

			safeWaitForChild4_2:FireServer(head, tablo8, {})

			if child then
				local mathFloor = math.floor(workspace:GetServerTimeNow() / 10 % 10) + 1
				local stringGsub = string.gsub("RE/RegisterHit", ".", function(arg)
					return string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(arg), mathFloor))
				end)
				local bit32Bxor = bit32.bxor(value + 909090, invokeServer * 2)

				cloneref(child):FireServer(stringGsub, bit32Bxor, head, tablo8)
			end
		end
	end,
}

					humanoidRootPart2 = v1312_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					end

					until alive or not _G.Level or not v1310.Parent or not Attack.Alive(v1310)

GetCurrentFloor = function()
	local getHRP = GetHRP()

	if not getHRP then
		return
	end

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map.Dungeon:GetChildren()) do
		local exitTeleporter = child:FindFirstChild("ExitTeleporter")

		if
			exitTeleporter
			and exitTeleporter:FindFirstChild("Root")
			and (getHRP.Position - exitTeleporter.Root.Position).Magnitude < 200
		then
			return exitTeleporter.Root
		end
	end
end

	while task.wait(0.3) do

					tp1Done = true
				end
			end
		else
			tp1Done = false
		end
	end
end)

						local exitTeleporter = child:FindFirstChild("ExitTeleporter")

						then
							getHRP.CFrame = exitTeleporter.Root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
							tp2Done = true

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		else
			tp2Done = false
		end
	end
end)
GetHighestFloor = function()
	local child2

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map.Dungeon:GetChildren()) do
		local toNumber = tonumber(child.Name)

		if toNumber and (not child2 or tonumber(child2.Name) < toNumber) then
			child2 = child
		end
	end

	return child2
end

end)
GetNearestExit = function()
	local getHRP = GetHRP()

	if not getHRP then
		return
	end

	local root
	local mathHuge = math.huge

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map.Dungeon:GetChildren()) do
		local exitTeleporter = child:FindFirstChild("ExitTeleporter")

		if exitTeleporter and exitTeleporter:FindFirstChild("Root") then
			local magnitude2 = (getHRP.Position - exitTeleporter.Root.Position).Magnitude

			if magnitude2 < mathHuge then
				mathHuge = magnitude2
				root = exitTeleporter.Root
			end
		end
	end

	return root
end

					GetHRP().CFrame = getNearestExit.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
					tp4Done = true
				end
			end
		else
			tp4Done = false
		end
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == "Nearest Aim" then
				local mathHuge = math.huge

				for _, player in pairs(players3:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer5 and player.Team ~= localPlayer5.Team and player.Character then
						if player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local magnitude2 = (
								player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
								- localPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
							).Magnitude

							if magnitude2 < mathHuge then
								mathHuge = magnitude2
								MousePos = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(Sec) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.AimCam then
				local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

				closestplayer = function()
					local mathHuge = math.huge
					local v
					local playerList, playerList2 = ply:GetPlayers()

					for _, v in next, playerList, playerList2 do
						if v ~= localPlayer5 and v.Character then
							if v.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and _G.AimCam and v.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
								local magnitude2 = (v.Character.Head.Position - localPlayer5.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude

								if magnitude2 < mathHuge then
									mathHuge = magnitude2
									v = v
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return v1370
				end

				while true do
					task.wait()
					CurrentCamera.CFrame =
						CFrame.new(CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, closestplayer().Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)

					if _G.AimCam == false then
						break
					elseif dist < Mag then
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	local tick2 = 0

	while task.wait() do
		if _G.AutoPlayerHunter then
			pcall(function()
				local main = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main

				if main.Quest.Visible == false then
					task.wait(0.5)

					if _G.AutoPlayerHunter then
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("PlayerHunter")
					end
				else
					local text = main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text

					for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Characters:GetChildren()) do
						if not _G.AutoPlayerHunter then
							break
						end

						if child ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and string.find(text, child.Name, 1, true) then
							local v1381 = false
							local v1382 = false

							repeat
								task.wait()

								local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local humanoid2 = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

								if not child.Parent or not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoid2 then
									v1382 = true

									break
								end

								if humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
									v1381 = true

									break
								end

								if AutoHaki then
									AutoHaki()
								end

								if EquipWeapon then
									EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
								end

								Useskill = true
								_tp(humanoidRootPart2.CFrame * CFrame.new(1, 7, 3))
								humanoidRootPart2.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
								game:GetService("VirtualUser"):CaptureController()
								game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1280, 672))
							until _G.AutoPlayerHunter == false

							Useskill = false

							if (v1381 or v1382) and _G.AutoPlayerHunter and tick() - tick2 > 5 then
								tick2 = tick()
								game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local userInputService2 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local runService2 = game:GetService("RunService")
local localPlayer6 = players.LocalPlayer
local p110 = false
local p111 = 50
local connection
local tablo8 = {
	f = 0,
	b = 0,
	l = 0,
	r = 0,
}
local bodyGyro
local bodyVelocity

updateControlsFromJoystick = function()
	local character2 = localPlayer6.Character
	local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if humanoid2 then
		local moveDirection = humanoid2.MoveDirection
		local cFrame3 = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame

		if cFrame3 and moveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
			local vectorToObjectSpace = cFrame3:VectorToObjectSpace(moveDirection)

			tablo8.f = vectorToObjectSpace.Z < -0.1 and 1 or vectorToObjectSpace.Z > 0.1 and -1 or 0
			tablo8.b = 0
			tablo8.l = vectorToObjectSpace.X < -0.1 and -1 or 0
			tablo8.r = vectorToObjectSpace.X > 0.1 and 1 or 0
		else
			tablo8.f = 0
			tablo8.b = 0
			tablo8.l = 0
			tablo8.r = 0
		end
	end
end
userInputService2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if not gameProcessed and p110 then
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
			tablo8.f = 1
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
			tablo8.b = -1
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
			tablo8.l = -1
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
			tablo8.r = 1
		end
	end
end)
userInputService2.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if p110 then
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W and tablo8.f == 1 then
			tablo8.f = 0
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S and tablo8.b == -1 then
			tablo8.b = 0
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A and tablo8.l == -1 then
			tablo8.l = 0
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D and tablo8.r == 1 then
			tablo8.r = 0
		end
	end
end)
setupMobileControls = function()
	local connection2

	connection2 = runService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if p110 then
			updateControlsFromJoystick()
		elseif connection2 then
			connection2:Disconnect()
		end
	end)
end
toggleFly = function(arg)
	p110 = arg

	if p110 then
		if not localPlayer6.Character then
			return
		end

		local humanoid2 = localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local torso

		if localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
			torso = localPlayer6.Character.Torso
		else
			torso = localPlayer6.Character.UpperTorso
		end

		if not humanoid2 or not torso then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(localPlayer6.Character:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				descendant.CanCollide = false
				descendant.Massless = true
			end
		end

		localPlayer6.Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if p110 then
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
					descendant.Massless = true
				end
			end
		end)
		bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro", Torso)
		bodyGyro.P = 90000
		bodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		bodyGyro.cframe = Torso.CFrame
		bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Torso)
		bodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		bodyVelocity.maxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		humanoid.PlatformStand = true
		setupMobileControls()
		connection = runService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not p110 or not localPlayer6.Character then
				return
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(localPlayer6.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanCollide then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			if tablo8.l + tablo8.r == 0 and tablo8.f + tablo8.b == 0 then
				bodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			else
				bodyVelocity.velocity = (
					workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (tablo8.f + tablo8.b)
					+ (
						workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame
							* CFrame.new(tablo8.l + tablo8.r, (tablo8.f + tablo8.b) * 0.2, 0).p
						- workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p
					)
				) * p111
			end

			bodyGyro.cframe = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame
		end)
	else
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		if localPlayer6.Character then
			local humanoid2 = localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid2 then
				humanoid2.PlatformStand = false
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(localPlayer6.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = true
					descendant.Massless = false
				end
			end

			if bodyGyro then
				bodyGyro:Destroy()
			end

			if bodyVelocity then
				bodyVelocity:Destroy()
			end
		end

		tablo8 = {
			f = 0,
			b = 0,
			l = 0,
			r = 0,
		}
	end
end
_G.toggleFly = toggleFly
updateFlySpeed = function(arg)
	p111 = arg
end
_G.updateFlySpeed = updateFlySpeed
NoCooldown = function()
	local dodge = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Dodge")
	local getGC, getGC2 = getgc()

	for _, v in next, getGC, getGC2 do
		if typeof(v) == "function" and getfenv(v).script == dodge then
			local next = next
			local getUpvalues, getUpvalues2 = getupvalues(v)

			for k, value2 in next, getUpvalues, getUpvalues2 do
				if tostring(value2) == "0.4" then
					setupvalue(v, k, 0)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
