ChestEsp = function()
	if ChestESP then
		local tagged = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("_ChestTagged")

		for _, v in ipairs(tagged) do
			pcall(function()
				local magnitude2 = (v:GetPivot().Position - localPlayer5.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude

				v:GetFullName():gsub("[^%w_]", "_")

				local chestEspAttachment = v:FindFirstChild("ChestEspAttachment")

				if not chestEspAttachment then
					local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")

					attachment.Name = "ChestEspAttachment"
					attachment.Parent = v
					attachment.Position = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

					local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

					billboardGui.Name = "NameEsp"
					billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
					billboardGui.Adornee = attachment
					billboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
					billboardGui.Parent = attachment

					local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

					textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
					textLabel.TextSize = 14
					textLabel.TextWrapped = true
					textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
					textLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
					textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
					textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
					textLabel.Parent = billboardGui
				end

				local nameEsp = chestEspAttachment and chestEspAttachment:FindFirstChild("NameEsp")

				if nameEsp then
					local mathFloor = math.floor(magnitude2 / 3)

					nameEsp.TextLabel.Text = string.format("[%s] %d M", v.Name:gsub("Label", ""), mathFloor)
				end
			end)
		end
	else
		for _, v in ipairs(game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("_ChestTagged")) do
			local chestEspAttachment = v:FindFirstChild("ChestEspAttachment")

			if chestEspAttachment then
				chestEspAttachment:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end
berriesEsp = function()
	if BerryEsp then
		local tagged = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("BerryBush")

		for _, v in ipairs(tagged) do
			pcall(function()
				local position = v.Parent:GetPivot().Position

				for _, attributeValue in pairs(v:GetAttributes()) do
					if attributeValue then
						local berryEspPart = "BerryEspPart_" .. attributeValue .. "_" .. tostring(position)
						local child2 = workspace:FindFirstChild(berryEspPart)

						if not child2 then
							child2 = Instance.new("Part")
							child2.Name = berryEspPart
							child2.Transparency = 1
							child2.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
							child2.Anchored = true
							child2.CanCollide = false
							child2.Parent = workspace
							child2.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
						end

						if not child2:FindFirstChild("NameEsp") then
							local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui", child2)

							billboardGui.Name = "NameEsp"
							billboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
							billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
							billboardGui.Adornee = child2
							billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

							local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)

							textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
							textLabel.TextSize = 14
							textLabel.TextWrapped = true
							textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
							textLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
							textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
							textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
						end

						local nameEsp = child2:FindFirstChild("NameEsp")
						local magnitude2 = (localPlayer5.Character.Head.Position - position).Magnitude / 3

						if nameEsp then
							nameEsp.TextLabel.Text = "[" .. attributeValue .. "]" .. " " .. math.round(magnitude2) .. " M"
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	else
		for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Part") then
				if child.Name:match("BerryEspPart_.*") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

				end

				if distributedGameTime then
					while distributedGameTime - workspace.DistributedGameTime < -300 do
						distributedGameTime += 5400
					end

					distributedGameTime2 = distributedGameTime - workspace.DistributedGameTime

					return distributedGameTime2 > 0 and distributedGameTime2 <= 180 and "Starts in " .. math.ceil(distributedGameTime2 / 60) .. "m"
				end
			elseif distributedGameTime then
				while distributedGameTime - workspace.DistributedGameTime < -300 do
					distributedGameTime += 5400
				end

				distributedGameTime2 = distributedGameTime - workspace.DistributedGameTime

				local mathCeil = distributedGameTime2 > 0 and distributedGameTime2 <= 180 and "Starts in " .. math.ceil(distributedGameTime2 / 60) .. "m"

				return mathCeil
			end

			return false
		end

		local vector3 = Vector3.new(-5542, 314, -2971)
		local osClock

		local function func()
			for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local humanoid2 = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local lower = child.Name:lower()

				if
					humanoidRootPart2
					and humanoid2
					and humanoid2.Health > 0
					and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - vector3).Magnitude <= 1200
					and not lower:find("indra")
					and not lower:find("boss")
					and child.Name ~= "Blank Buddy"
				then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end
		local function func()
			if not World3 then
				return false
			end

			if func() then
				osClock = nil

				return "Active"
			end

			return osClock ~= nil and os.clock() - osClock < 90 and "Incoming"
		end

		local new = result.new({
			Url = t43.URL,
			Id = t43.ID,
			Sign = function(arg, arg2)
				return v1506(arg .. arg2 .. t43.SECRET)
			end,
			Teleport = function(arg)
				ReplicatedStorage4.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer("teleport", arg.job_id)
			end,
			OnNotify = function(arg, arg2)
				pcall(function()
					game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
						Title = arg,
						Text = arg2,
						Duration = 5,
					})
				end)
			end,
		})

		new:AddEvents({
			{
				Key = "fullmoon",
				Name = "Full Moon",
				MaxAge = 5,
				Check = function()
					return func() == 5
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "nearfullmoon",
				Name = "Near Full Moon",
				MaxAge = 5,
				Check = function()
					return func() == 4
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "mirage",
				Name = "Mirage Island",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					return Locations:FindFirstChild("Mirage Island") ~= nil
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "kitsune",
				Name = "Kitsune Island",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					return workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland") ~= nil
						or Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island") ~= nil
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "prehistoric",
				Name = "Prehistoric Island",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					return Locations:FindFirstChild("Prehistoric Island") ~= nil
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "frozen",
				Name = "Frozen Dimension",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					return Locations:FindFirstChild("Frozen Dimension") ~= nil
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "elites",
				Name = "Elite Hunter",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					if not World3 then
						return false
					end

					for _, v in ipairs({
						"Urban",
						"Deandre",
						"Diablo",
					}) do
						if v1539(v) then
							return v
						end
					end

					return false
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "factory",
				Name = "Factory Raid",
				MaxAge = 5,
				Check = func,
			},
			{
				Key = "castleraid",
				Name = "Castle Raid",
				MaxAge = 5,
				Check = func,
			},
			{
				Key = "legendarysword",
				Name = "Legendary Sword",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Items = {
					"Shizu",
					"Oroshi",
					"Saishi",
				},
				Check = function()
					if not World2 then
						return false
					end

					local invokeServer = ReplicatedStorage4.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LegendarySwordDealer", "1")

					return type(invokeServer) == "string" and invokeServer ~= "" and invokeServer or false
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "hakicolor",
				Name = "Haki Color",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					local invokeServer = ReplicatedStorage4.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("ColorsDealer", "1")

					if
						type(invokeServer) == "string"
						and #invokeServer >= 3
						and #invokeServer <= 30
						and invokeServer:match("^[%w' %-]+$")
					then
						return invokeServer
					end

					return false
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "fourhours",
				Name = "4 Hours Server",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					return workspace.DistributedGameTime >= 14400
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "fruits",
				Name = "Spawned Fruit",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					local tablo11 = {}

					for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Model") then
							if child.Name:match("^Fruit%s*$") then
								local v1546_2 = v1546(child)

								if not table.find(tablo11, v1546_2) then
									table.insert(tablo11, v1546_2)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return func(t49)
				end,
			},
			{
				Key = "boss",
				Name = "Boss",
				MaxAge = 10,
				Check = function()
					local tablo11 = {}

					for _, v in ipairs(tablo9_2) do
						if v1539(v) then
							table.insert(tablo11, tablo10[v] or v)
						end
					end

					return func(tablo11)
				end,
			},
		})

		local tablo11 = {}
		local tablo12 = {
			{
				"trouble has broken out at the tavern",

		local function func(arg)
			local lower = tostring(arg or ""):gsub("<[^>]->", ""):lower()

			if not World3 then
				for _, v in ipairs(tablo12) do
					if not lower:find(v[1], 1, true) then
						continue
					end

					tablo11[v[2]] = os.clock()

					return
				end

				return
			end

			if lower:find("pirates have been spotted", 1, true) then
				if os.clock() - (osClock or -60) > 60 then
					osClock = os.clock()
					task.spawn(pcall, new.Step, new)
				end
			end
		end

		if World1 or World3 then
			task.spawn(function()
				local textChannels = game:GetService("TextChatService"):WaitForChild("TextChannels", 30)
				local rBXGeneral = textChannels and textChannels:WaitForChild("RBXGeneral", 30)

				if rBXGeneral then
					rBXGeneral.MessageReceived:Connect(function(arg)
						if arg.TextSource == nil then
							func(arg.Text)
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				local playerGui = localPlayer5:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 30)
				local notifications = playerGui and playerGui:WaitForChild("Notifications", 30)
				local notificationStack = notifications and notifications:WaitForChild("NotificationStack", 30)

				if notificationStack then
					notificationStack.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
						if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") then
							task.defer(function()
								func(descendant.Text)
							end)
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
		end

		local tablo13 = {}

		for _, v in ipairs(tablo12) do
			table.insert(tablo13, v[2])
		end

		new:AddEvent({
			Key = "secret",
			Name = "Secret Quest",
			MaxAge = 10,
			Items = tablo13,
			Witness = true,
			Check = function()
				if not World1 then
					return false
				end

				local tablo14 = {}

				for k, v in pairs(tablo11) do
					if os.clock() - v < 600 then
						table.insert(tablo14, k)
					end
				end

				return func(tablo14)
			end,
		})
		task.spawn(function()
			local request, request2 = new:Request("GET", "/dictionary/fruits")

			if request and type(request2) == "table" then
				for _, v in ipairs(request2) do
					if type(v) == "table" and type(v.mesh_id) == "string" and type(v.name) == "string" then
						t46[v.mesh_id] = v.name

						local match = v.mesh_id:match("%d+")

						if match then
							t46[match] = v.name
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		new.Thread = task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(math.random() * 5)

			while Core.ServerReporter == new do
				new:SetEnabled(getgenv().ShareServerEvents ~= false)
				pcall(new.Step, new)
				task.wait(15)
			end
		end)
		Core.ServerReporter = new
		Core.ServerEvents = new.Events
		Core.ListEventServers = function(arg)
			return new:ListServers(arg or 10)
		end
		Core.JoinEventServer = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			new:JoinEvent(arg, arg2, arg3)
		end
		Core.JoinServerList = function(arg, arg2)
			new:JoinServerList(arg, arg2)
		end
	else
		warn("[Core] Server Finder unavailable: " .. tostring(result))
	end
